cua-driver-rs-v0.49.3cua-driver-rs: v0.49.3 Prerelease2026-11-13T14:06:03Z

Cua Driver 0.49.3

Summary

This release includes 1 fix.

Fixes

  • uninstall-local stops app-bundle daemons and removes release markers. (#7615)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

Cua Driver 0.49.2

Summary

This release includes 2 fixes.

Fixes

  • make Linux browser prepare language independent. (#7587) Thanks @loonghao.
  • cover foreground typing into a minimized Screen Sharing window. (#7594)

Contributors

Thanks to @loonghao for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

cua-driver-rs-v0.49.0cua-driver-rs: v0.49.0 Prerelease2026-11-13T07:30:09Z

Cua Driver 0.49.0

Summary

This release adds 1 features and includes 2 fixes.

Features

  • refusals say what will work instead. (#7539)

Fixes

  • place the session cursor for desktop-scope keyboard actions. (#7527)
  • read hidden Chromium windows as they are now. (#7561)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

Automated immutable nightly built from an exact main commit. Nightlies are opt-in, may be less stable than regular releases, and never replace stable update discovery.

cua-driver-rs-v0.48.0cua-driver-rs: v0.48.0 Prerelease2026-11-13T05:11:56Z

Cua Driver 0.48.0

Summary

This release adds 1 features and includes 3 fixes.

Features

  • remove picture-in-picture preview and deprecated session tools. (#7481)

Fixes

  • restore Windows web harness e2e coverage. (#7537)
  • do not strip proc-macro dylibs in release builds. (#7540)
  • run_steps takes its window from element tokens and accepts flat step arguments. (#7553) Thanks ec2-user.

Contributors

Thanks to ec2-user for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

opendatalab/MinerU ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
mineru-4.2.2-releasedMinerU 4.2.2 Stable2026-11-13T14:02:54Z

4.2.2

  • 完善本地 VLM 引擎的故障传播与生命周期管理。统一消费 LMDeploy、vLLM、llama.cpp 和 MLX 的公共健康与失效契约；确认引擎失效后停止复用缓存实例，并通知关联宿主，避免把全局故障当作普通识别失败而静默降级。普通单片识别失败仍保留既有回退。
  • 增强解析服务的健康检查与任务清理。周期探测已加载且支持健康检查的运行时；暂时不可用时拒绝新推理，恢复健康后重新开放。确认引擎死亡时，健康与能力接口返回明确错误，运行及排队任务按既有边界清理，恢复由宿主管理。完善关闭期间的在途探测、取消和资源释放顺序。
  • 修复批量解析的输入与错误处理。使用内容签名和容器识别验证文件，避免 Office 锁文件阻断后续文档，同时保留名称以 ~$ 开头的合法文档。明确的损坏、加密、资源限制、输入缺失及权限错误记录为单项失败并继续；引擎、服务或输出故障停止后续处理。摘要分别报告成功、跳过、失败和未处理数量，部分失败返回非零退出码。
  • 修复批量输出路径判断。目录和多文件输入始终采用目录输出，只有一个文档时也保持相同语义。支持已有或新建的 results.v1、results.md 等带点目录，拒绝将批量结果写入已有普通文件；显式单文件输出继续支持覆盖目标文件。
  • 明确阅读与导出的职责。mineru read 的 Markdown 输出用于渐进阅读，保留 stdout、JSON 和续读提示；--output PATH 仅用于图片。保存当前文本片段可使用重定向或 SDK，完整导出指定页范围使用 mineru parse ... -o ...，避免把受阅读预算限制的片段误当成完整产物。
  • 改善服务启动与退出体验。服务成功监听后打印可直接访问的 API 文档地址；正常 Ctrl+C 退出不再显示中断堆栈。移除未实现的 webhook 能力声明及对应文档。
  • 更新运行时依赖。最低版本调整为 mineru-llama-cpp 0.2.1 和 mineru-vl-utils 2.0.9，与本次公共生命周期契约保持一致。

兼容性提示。原有 mineru read <locator> -o part.md 需要改为 stdout 重定向或 SDK 保存；需要完整产物时使用 mineru parse <path> --pages <range> -o result.md。批量调用方应根据新的结果摘要和退出码区分完整成功、部分失败和全局中止。

4.2.2

  • Improve local VLM failure propagation and lifecycle management. Consume shared health and failure contracts for LMDeploy, vLLM, llama.cpp, and MLX. Confirmed engine failure invalidates cached instances and notifies their hosts, preventing a global outage from silently becoming a local recognition fallback. Ordinary per-crop failures retain the existing fallback behavior.
  • Strengthen parse-server health checks and task cleanup. Periodically probe initialized runtimes that support health checks. Temporary unavailability rejects new inference until health recovers. Confirmed engine death produces explicit health and capability errors and cleans up running and queued tasks through the existing lifecycle boundaries; recovery remains the host's responsibility. Improve the ordering of active probes, cancellation, and resource release during shutdown.
  • Correct batch input validation and error handling. Validate file content signatures and containers so Office lock files do not stop subsequent documents, while valid documents beginning with ~$ remain supported. Report known corrupt, encrypted, resource-limited, missing, or inaccessible inputs as individual failures and continue. Engine, service, and output failures stop the batch. Summaries distinguish succeeded, skipped, failed, and unprocessed inputs; partial failure returns a nonzero exit code.
  • Correct batch output-path handling. Directory and multiple-file inputs always produce directory output, even when only one document remains. Accept existing and new dotted directory names such as results.v1 and results.md, and reject existing regular files as batch destinations. Explicit single-file output continues to support overwriting its destination.
  • Clarify reading and exporting. Markdown from mineru read remains a progressive stdout or JSON read with continuation hints; --output PATH is reserved for images. Save the current text fragment through redirection or the SDK, and use mineru parse ... -o ... to export a complete page range without the reading budget.
  • Improve service startup and shutdown. Print directly accessible API documentation URLs after successful binding, avoid interrupt tracebacks on normal Ctrl+C shutdown, and remove unimplemented webhook capability declarations and documentation.
  • Update runtime dependencies. Require mineru-llama-cpp 0.2.1 and mineru-vl-utils 2.0.9 or newer compatible versions for the shared lifecycle contracts.

Compatibility note. Replace mineru read <locator> -o part.md with stdout redirection or SDK saving. For complete exports, use mineru parse <path> --pages <range> -o result.md. Batch callers should interpret the result summary and exit code to distinguish full success, partial failure, and an aborted batch.

升级 / Upgrade

pip install -U "mineru==4.2.2"

View release →

v0.5.5 Stable2026-11-13T13:13:11Z

中文

主要更新

  • PostgreSQL 部署选项：新增 pgvector 向量后端（storage.vectordb.backend: "pgvector"，支持精确检索和 HNSW 检索，需要安装 openviking[pgvector]）和 PostgreSQL PathLock（storage.agfs.pathlock.provider: "postgres"）。多进程、多主机部署不依赖 Redis，也可以共享向量数据和路径锁租约。#7516、#7481
  • 多节点协调：新增 storage.agfs.coordination（memory / sqlite / cache），统一负责 Watch 选主和语义任务版本；只有已持久化的工作才会取代旧任务，按 key 的状态保留一天后过期。#7466
  • 快照路径历史索引：snapshot/log?paths=... 改用派生索引查找改动过指定路径的提交，默认为 SQLite，多实例部署可选 MySQL，设为 none 则保持原来的扫描方式。快照提交、文件内容和 API 响应都不变。#7505
  • Gateway Context Profile：新增 recall_peer_scope（all / actor），可把召回限制在当前 peer；新增 capture_memory_types，限制 capture 抽取的记忆类型；capture 会话保留用户自己的记忆策略。#7389、#7367、#7429
  • 使用次数统计与批量标签：set_tags 的 append 模式支持 k+=number / k-=number（十进制加减，key 不存在时从 0 开始），可用于计数；新增 POST /api/v1/content/batch_set_tags，一次 1–50 个 URI，返回成功项 items 和失败项 failed，部分失败时仍返回 200。CLI（ov attrs batch-set-tags）和 Python、TypeScript、Go SDK 均已提供。#7405
  • 静态 soul.md 与预置策略：memories/soul.md 改为可编辑的静态 Agent 指令，VikingBot 每轮优先读取 OpenViking 中的 soul.md；开启 server.user_config_defaults.preset_global_policy 后，初始化用户时会创建内置的 GLOBAL_POLICY.md（默认关闭）。#7328、#7512
  • 导入与检索：首次非严格导入允许部分成功（此前任一文件失败即整体失败）；支持 Wiki 托管的飞书文件和原生飞书幻灯片导入；远程 PathGlob 由 storage.vectordb.enable_path_glob 控制，未设置时沿用各适配器的默认值。#7427、#7153、#7461

发布版本

主包 openviking==0.5.5、Python SDK openviking-sdk==0.1.20、独立 CLI 0.5.3。batch_set_tags 和 ov attrs batch-set-tags 需要 0.5.5 服务端。

pip install --upgrade 'openviking==0.5.5' 'openviking-sdk==0.1.20'

升级与迁移

  1. Gateway 默认开放全部 OpenViking 工具（含写工具）：Context Profile 的 disabled_tools 默认值改为空，默认开放全部工具，包括 remember、write、edit、add_resource、add_skill、forget、set_acl、cancel_watch 这 8 个会修改数据的工具（之前的版本默认关闭它们）；之后新增的工具也默认开放。已保存的 Profile 和进行中的会话不受影响；没有保存过 default Profile 的账户，以及 gateway 配置里未写 disabled_tools 的 default_profile，现在会得到全部工具。PUT /admin/policies/{id} 是全量替换：请求里省略 disabled_tools 会把已保存 Profile 的写工具全部打开。要保留原来的选择，请在 default_profile.disabled_tools 中显式列出这些工具，并在每次更新 Profile 时提交完整的 disabled_tools。#7436
  2. X-OpenViking-Context 只能收窄工具权限：请求头里的 disabled_tools 会追加到 Profile 已排除的工具上，gateway_tools 也不能重新开启 Profile 关闭的工具。原来靠 {"disabled_tools":[]} 或 {"gateway_tools":true} 拿到额外工具的客户端，需要改为修改 Profile。#7446
  3. soul / identity 不再自动抽取：这两种记忆类型已经下线，已持久化的策略和部署覆盖里即使写了也会被忽略；账户记忆模板接口只返回 4 个可编辑模板。已有的 soul.md 和 identity.md 保持原样；缺少 soul.md 的用户会在初始化时或下一次 self 记忆提交时，用默认内容创建。自动记忆更新不能修改 soul.md 和 identity.md，包含此类操作的整批更新会被拒绝。#7328
  4. Kafka 上报配置迁入 ov.conf（breaking 配置变更）：Nacos 和外部 JSON 的读取已删除。server.observability.reporting 移除了 config_file 和 nacos_* 字段，NACOS_CONFIG_FILE、NACOS_SERVER_ADDR 等环境变量也不再读取；改为读取 reporting.kafka，并需要同时开启 reporting.enabled 和 reporting.telemetry.enabled。旧字段不会让启动失败，但不迁移就不会再上报。原来通过 Nacos 或外部 JSON 配置的部署，需要把 kafka、telemetry 两段迁入 ov.conf 后重启。部署归属可写在 reporting.telemetry.deployment，OV_METRICS_ACCOUNT_ID、OV_RESOURCE_ID、OV_REGION 仍作为回退值。#7491
  5. Skill 更新不再备份和回滚：更新时直接替换旧包，失败或取消后文件、索引和隐私配置停在失败时的状态，不会恢复旧版本。SKILL.md 和 .source.json 会保留到被覆盖为止，可以直接重试更新。#7509
  6. 首次非严格导入可以部分成功：之前只要有一个文件失败，目录导入就整体失败（不论 strict）。现在 strict=false（默认）时，新目标的首次导入如果有部分读取或解析失败，会提交成功的部分：任务状态为完成，结果为 status="partial"、import_complete=false，失败项列在 failed_files；只检查任务状态的调用方需要同时检查 import_complete。部分导入不会推进源 checkpoint，Watch 本轮记为 partial；源认证失败仍整体失败。strict=true 仍拒绝不完整的提交；更新已有资源时结果不完整会返回 FAILED_PRECONDITION，旧内容保持不变。#7427
  7. Git 导入网络检查：Git 预检、clone 和 fetch 不再跟随 HTTP 重定向，请使用最终的仓库地址；GitHub/GitLab ZIP 下载仍跟随重定向，但每个目标地址都会校验。回环、链路本地、未指定、组播、保留地址和已知云元数据地址一律拒绝，代码托管白名单和 allow_private_networks=true 也不能放行。#7441
  8. 隐私配置写接口遇锁冲突立即返回 409：显式的 HTTP 修改不再等待配置锁（原来最多等 30 秒），返回 CONFLICT，details 中 conflict_type 为 path_busy、retryable 为 true，客户端应退避重试；内部 Session/Skill 抽取仍按原来的方式等待。#7553
  9. 快照路径索引默认启用：默认在 {storage.workspace}/_system/snapshot/log/snapshot_log.db 创建 SQLite 索引（每个节点各自一份）。升级后每个账户、分支的第一次提交会在后台回填全部历史，历史较长的部署会在每个节点上产生一次 CPU 和 IO 开销。多实例部署请配置 git.snapshot.log.provider: "mysql" 和 OPENVIKING_SNAPSHOT_LOG_DSN：服务启动时建表，数据库账号需要建表权限，DSN 环境变量缺失时服务无法启动。设为 none 可关闭。#7505
  10. watch_leader_provider 已弃用：下一个版本将移除。请改为设置 storage.agfs.coordination.provider，并删除旧字段；cache 需要顶层 cache 配置。参与协调的节点需要使用相同的后端和顶层 service_name，建议同时升级，避免新旧节点长时间混跑。使用 cache 时语义任务版本在入队时由 Redis 分配，Redis 不可用会导致入队失败。#7466
  11. 标签加减运算改变了以 + / - 结尾的 key 的含义：set_tags 的 append 模式中，key 以 + 或 - 结尾的标签按十进制加减处理（如 count+=1 把 count 加 1），不再作为普通标签保存；c++=1 现在是给 c+ 加 1。值不是数字（如 x+=abc）或已有值不是数字时返回 InvalidArgumentError。append 模式中的非法标签（如缺少 =）不再被静默丢弃，而是让请求失败。#7405
  12. 依赖：LiteLLM 固定为 >=1.103.10,<1.103.11，修复 Responses 非流式回复中叙述与工具调用被拆开的问题；新增可选依赖 openviking[pgvector]。#7313、#7516
  13. React 组件：@openviking/react 独立发版，本范围内有多处 breaking 接口变更，包括 WikiDashboard 操作改由宿主定义、TUI 快捷命令预设、FilePreview/FileBrowser 操作统一、公共组件改名、FileTree 的 renderDashboard 改为 renderRowTrailing、Console modes 以及移除 @openviking/react/modules/viking-bot 入口。以源码方式集成的项目请对照这些 PR 迁移。#7435、#7462、#7475、#7506、#7537、#7559

修复

  • 记忆：记忆工具会展开 viking://~/memories/... 别名；跨会话的写入和删除合并规划，避免最终冲突检查失败；训练删除或合并历史经验页时保留链接并对邻居页加锁；已合并的记忆标签不再重复解析；俄罗斯所有时区都映射为 ru。#7417、#7392、#7384、#7500、#7486
  • 存储与 Watch：同步失败会向上报告，不再在不完整同步后清理暂存内容或重放部分源树；增量导入分页不再漏读，并按 URI 补查索引；文件模式的 Watch 状态原样持久化，不再触发 Privacy 大小上限。#4786、#7501、#7551
  • 解析：AnyDoc 输出能解析脚注、尾注中指向书签的链接，并保留段落中只含 - / = 的字面文本。#7202、#7325
  • 模型与 CLI：额度耗尽归类为配额错误；CLI 设置了可识别的 OPENVIKING_LANG 时跳过首次语言初始化；同步请求的任务轮询间隔上限为 1 秒。#7308、#7439、#7572
  • Gateway 与插件：修复 Windows 上 SQLite 存储初始化；Codex 插件启动补传时使用历史会话自己的 peer；Pi 扩展按分支记录 capture 进度，恢复会话后不再重复上传历史。#7474、#6406、#5855

其他更新

Console、FileBrowser、文件预览、目录引导、VikingBot 会话、经验面板和只读版本历史均有更新，FileBrowser 搜索支持四种检索模式，新增 CodeMirror 编辑。React 包已发布 0.1.9 和 0.1.10，main 上的改动自动发布 beta。另有 ragfs 的 git 服务、锁管理器和 Redis provider 模块化重构，数据库契约测试改为对真实 MySQL 和 Redis 运行，以及 Responses 认证和实时上下文入门文档。#7344、#7438、#7460、#7472、#7490、#7498、#7499、#7510、#7513、#7523、#7524、#7536、#7541、#7554、#7556、#7557、#7522、#7542、#7445、#7442、#7549、#7519、#7314、#7573

English

Highlights

  • PostgreSQL deployment options: A pgvector vector backend (storage.vectordb.backend: "pgvector", with exact or HNSW search; install openviking[pgvector]) and PostgreSQL PathLock (storage.agfs.pathlock.provider: "postgres") let multi-process and multi-host deployments share vector data and path-lock leases without Redis. #7516, #7481
  • Multi-node coordination: The new storage.agfs.coordination setting (memory / sqlite / cache) handles Watch leadership and semantic task revisions. Only persisted work supersedes older tasks, and per-key state expires after one day. #7466
  • Snapshot path history index: snapshot/log?paths=... uses a derived index to find the commits that touched a path. SQLite is the default, MySQL is available for multi-instance deployments, and none keeps the original scan. Snapshot commits, stored content and API responses are unchanged. #7505
  • Gateway context profiles: recall_peer_scope (all / actor) can restrict recall to the current peer, capture_memory_types limits which memory types capture extracts, and capture sessions preserve the user's own memory policy. #7389, #7367, #7429
  • Usage counters and batch tags: append mode in set_tags accepts k+=number / k-=number patches (decimal arithmetic; a missing key starts at 0), which can serve as counters. POST /api/v1/content/batch_set_tags accepts 1-50 URIs and returns successful items and failed entries; a partial failure still returns 200. Available in the CLI (ov attrs batch-set-tags) and the Python, TypeScript and Go SDKs. #7405
  • Static soul.md and preset policy: memories/soul.md is now an editable static agent instruction file, and VikingBot reads it from OpenViking on each turn. server.user_config_defaults.preset_global_policy (off by default) creates the packaged GLOBAL_POLICY.md during user initialization. #7328, #7512
  • Import and retrieval: First non-strict imports can now partially succeed (previously any failed file failed the whole import); Feishu imports support Wiki-backed files and native Slides; remote PathGlob is controlled by storage.vectordb.enable_path_glob, which falls back to each adapter's default when unset. #7427, #7153, #7461

Published versions

Main package openviking==0.5.5, Python SDK openviking-sdk==0.1.20, and standalone CLI 0.5.3. batch_set_tags and ov attrs batch-set-tags require a 0.5.5 server.

pip install --upgrade 'openviking==0.5.5' 'openviking-sdk==0.1.20'

Upgrade and migration

  1. Gateway profiles enable every OpenViking tool by default, including write tools. The default disabled_tools is now empty, so remember, write, edit, add_resource, add_skill, forget, set_acl and cancel_watch, which earlier releases disabled by default, are enabled, and tools added later are enabled too. Saved profiles and ongoing conversations keep their tools. Accounts without a saved default profile, and a gateway default_profile that does not set disabled_tools, now get every tool. PUT /admin/policies/{id} replaces the whole profile: omitting disabled_tools in the request enables every write tool on a saved profile. To keep the previous selection, list these tools explicitly in default_profile.disabled_tools and always send the complete disabled_tools when updating a profile. #7436
  2. X-OpenViking-Context can only narrow tool access. disabled_tools in the header adds to the profile's exclusions, and gateway_tools cannot turn tools back on. Clients that relied on {"disabled_tools":[]} or {"gateway_tools":true} to get extra tools should change the profile instead. #7446
  3. soul and identity are no longer extracted. Both memory types are retired and ignored in persisted policies and deployment overrides. The account memory-template API lists 4 editable templates. Existing soul.md and identity.md files are left unchanged. A missing soul.md is created from the defaults during initialization or at the next self-memory commit. Automatic memory updates cannot modify soul.md or identity.md; a batch containing such an operation is rejected. #7328
  4. Kafka reporting is configured in ov.conf (breaking configuration change). Reading from Nacos or an external JSON file has been removed: server.observability.reporting drops config_file and the nacos_* fields, and NACOS_CONFIG_FILE, NACOS_SERVER_ADDR and related environment variables are no longer read. Reporting now reads reporting.kafka and requires both reporting.enabled and reporting.telemetry.enabled. The old fields do not stop the server from starting, but reporting stops until the configuration is migrated. Deployments configured through Nacos or an external JSON file must move the kafka and telemetry sections into ov.conf and restart. Deployment ownership can be set in reporting.telemetry.deployment; OV_METRICS_ACCOUNT_ID, OV_RESOURCE_ID and OV_REGION remain fallback values. #7491
  5. Skill updates no longer back up or roll back. An update replaces the old package directly. After a failure or cancellation, files, index records and privacy configuration stay as they were at the point of failure, and the old version is not restored. SKILL.md and .source.json are kept until overwritten, so a failed update can be retried. #7509
  6. First non-strict imports can partially succeed. Previously any failed file failed the whole directory import, regardless of strict. With strict=false (the default), a first import into a new target now commits the successful part: the task completes with status="partial", import_complete=false and the failed items in failed_files, so callers that only check the task status should also check import_complete. A partial import does not advance the source checkpoint, and the Watch run ends as partial; source authentication failures still fail the import. strict=true still rejects incomplete commits, and an incomplete update to an existing resource returns FAILED_PRECONDITION and leaves the old content in place. #7427
  7. Git import network checks. Git preflight, clone and fetch no longer follow HTTP redirects, so use the final repository URL. GitHub/GitLab ZIP downloads still follow redirects and validate each destination. Loopback, link-local, unspecified, multicast, reserved and known cloud-metadata addresses are always rejected, even for allowlisted code hosts or with allow_private_networks=true. #7441
  8. Privacy configuration writes return 409 on lock conflicts instead of waiting (previously up to 30 seconds). The error is CONFLICT with conflict_type: "path_busy" and retryable: true in details; clients should back off and retry. Internal session and skill extraction still waits. #7553
  9. The snapshot path index is enabled by default. It creates a SQLite database at {storage.workspace}/_system/snapshot/log/snapshot_log.db on each node. After the upgrade, the first commit on each account and branch backfills the full history in the background, which costs CPU and IO once per node on deployments with long histories. For multi-instance deployments, set git.snapshot.log.provider: "mysql" and OPENVIKING_SNAPSHOT_LOG_DSN: the table is created at startup, so the database user needs CREATE privileges, and the server does not start if the DSN variable is missing. Set none to disable the index. #7505
  10. watch_leader_provider is deprecated and will be removed in the next version. Set storage.agfs.coordination.provider and remove the old field; cache requires the top-level cache configuration. Participating nodes must share the backend and the top-level service_name; upgrade them together rather than running old and new nodes side by side for long. With cache, semantic task revisions are allocated in Redis at enqueue time, so enqueueing fails when Redis is unavailable. #7466
  11. Tag arithmetic changes the meaning of keys ending in + or -. In append mode of set_tags, a tag whose key ends in + or - is applied as decimal addition or subtraction (count+=1 adds 1 to count) instead of being stored as an ordinary tag, so c++=1 now adds 1 to c+. A non-numeric operand (such as x+=abc) or a non-numeric existing value returns InvalidArgumentError. Invalid tags in append mode (for example, without =) are no longer silently dropped; the request fails. #7405
  12. Dependencies: LiteLLM is pinned to >=1.103.10,<1.103.11, which keeps narration and function calls together in non-streaming Responses replies. The new optional extra openviking[pgvector] is available. #7313, #7516
  13. React components: @openviking/react is released independently, and this range contains several breaking API changes: host-defined WikiDashboard actions, TUI quick-command presets, unified FilePreview/FileBrowser actions, renamed public components, FileTree's renderDashboard renamed to renderRowTrailing, Console modes, and removal of the @openviking/react/modules/viking-bot entry. Source integrations should migrate according to these PRs. #7435, #7462, #7475, #7506, #7537, #7559

Fixes

  • Memory: Memory tools expand viking://~/memories/... aliases. Cross-session writes and deletes are planned together, so the final conflict check no longer fails. Training keeps links and locks neighbouring pages when it deletes or merges historical experience pages. Merged memory tags are not parsed again, and every Russian time zone maps to ru. #7417, #7392, #7384, #7500, #7486
  • Storage and Watches: Failed syncs are reported instead of being followed by staged-content cleanup or a replay of a partially moved source tree. Incremental-import pagination no longer misses entries and looks up indexes by URI. File-mode Watch state is persisted verbatim and no longer hits the Privacy size limit. #4786, #7501, #7551
  • Parsing: AnyDoc output resolves bookmark links found in notes and preserves literal - / = lines in paragraphs. #7202, #7325
  • Models and CLI: Exhausted credit balances are classified as quota errors. The CLI skips first-run language setup when a recognized OPENVIKING_LANG is set. Synchronous task waits poll at least once per second. #7308, #7439, #7572
  • Gateway and plugins: SQLite stores initialize on Windows. The Codex plugin's startup sweep keeps each historical session's own peer, and the Pi extension tracks capture progress per branch, so resumed sessions no longer re-upload history. #7474, #6406, #5855

Other updates

Console, FileBrowser, file previews, directory guidance, VikingBot conversations, the experience dashboard and read-only version history are updated. FileBrowser search offers four retrieval modes, and file previews gain CodeMirror editing. React 0.1.9 and 0.1.10 are published, and changes on main now publish beta builds automatically. This release also modularizes the ragfs git service, lock manager and Redis provider, runs the database contract tests against real MySQL and Redis, and adds documentation for Responses authentication and a live-context walkthrough. #7344, #7438, #7460, #7472, #7490, #7498, #7499, #7510, #7513, #7523, #7524, #7536, #7541, #7554, #7556, #7557, #7522, #7542, #7445, #7442, #7549, #7519, #7314, #7573

首次贡献者 / New contributors

感谢 / Thanks to daniil4545 (#7486) and mmhh256 (#7474).

anomalyco/opencode ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.3.0 Stable2026-11-13T08:55:07Z

Highlights

  • Background Subagents by Default: Subagents now return immediately, continue in the background, and report back whenever they become idle. You can keep working in the parent session while multiple subagents run concurrently. (#63382)
  • Quieter Transcripts by Default: The TUI now summarizes each run of tool calls and thoughts into one collapsible activity row, so the answer is easier to follow. To see every step again, set Verbosity to medium or high in settings, or set session.verbosity in your TUI config; an existing setting is kept. (#63207)
  • Jump to an Earlier Prompt: In the desktop app, open Jump to message from the command palette or press Cmd+Alt+G (Ctrl+Alt+G on Windows and Linux) to search prompts across the current session and jump directly to one. (#63585)

Core

Improvements

  • Subagents now always run in the background, return their session ID immediately, and report results to their parent whenever they become idle. (#63382)
  • Added experimental support for MCP events, behind the mcp_events flag. (#63254, #63261, #63269)
  • Shell commands now survive server handoffs and keep their output when interrupted.
  • Added experimental support for moving a shell command to the background when its timeout expires, behind the shell_timeout_background flag. With the flag on, an explicit timeout on a background command stops it. (#63024, #63703)
  • Local Windows shell commands now run through the process daemon.
  • Added support for freeform tool input on compatible models.
  • Added LM Studio decision-model support.
  • Agents are now told when their session is a fork. Thanks @hasta84. (#63235)
  • Tool output truncation now keeps the beginning of the output and preserves a usable preview if the full output cannot be saved.
  • Tail reads no longer load the entire file into memory.
  • Providers can present a client certificate for gateways that require mutual TLS. Set settings.tls with cert, key and an optional passphrase on the provider; values accept {file:} and {env:} like apiKey. (#61832)
  • When a subagent goes idle while its own background work is still running, its report to the parent now says it is still running, so the parent waits for the final report instead of treating the partial answer as done. (#63675)
  • Code Mode gets simpler structured results from file and skill tools: write and edit return their confirmation as a string, patch returns an empty result, and skill returns the skill's directory and content. (#63616, #63635, #63618, #63623)

Bug fixes

  • Fixed case-only file renames in patches on case-insensitive filesystems.
  • Fixed stale thinking being retained after Claude Agent prompts or tools change.
  • Fixed directly driven child sessions not resuming after a restart.
  • Fixed plugin replacement stopping when cleanup of an earlier plugin fails.
  • Fixed MCP OAuth refreshes and localhost redirects when a server rewrites 127.0.0.1.
  • Fixed saved API keys being omitted from local-model discovery.
  • Local model discovery (Ollama, LM Studio, vLLM) waits up to 15 seconds per request instead of 1, so remote or tunnelled endpoints list their models, and a slow endpoint no longer delays other endpoints or config reloads. (#63700)
  • In Code Mode, calling a namespace as if it were a tool now suggests up to three real tools under it. (#63697)
  • Fixed retryable provider streams losing completed work when interrupted.
  • Fixed GitHub Copilot requests using the wrong endpoint before models finish loading.
  • Fixed exhausted request rate limits being treated as terminal. Thanks @Kyle-j-bush. (#59816)
  • Fixed LM Studio models with a null context length and accepted decision-type models. Thanks @tomzhu1024. (#56214)

TUI

Improvements

  • Transcript verbosity now defaults to low, which summarizes each run of tools and thoughts into one activity row. A session.verbosity you already set is kept. (#63207)
  • Session IDs found in messages can now be opened directly.
  • Activity tabs are clickable and open the correct view.
  • Shell mode has its own prompt color under the new designs.

Bug fixes

  • Restored pasted text when undo brings back its placeholder.
  • Restored the live draft after browsing prompt history.
  • Fixed copying a paste from within the prompt selection.
  • Preserved the Mini composer caret when closing the command palette.
  • Prevented image bytes from being retained in transcript storage when image previews are disabled.
  • Fixed paste-chip contrast across light and dark themes.
  • Fixed plugin-provided themes updating live.
  • Session lists now open at the top rather than at the current session.
  • Mini scrollback now shows the subagent card instead of obsolete launch instructions.

Desktop

Improvements

  • Added Jump to message to the command palette with Cmd+Alt+G / Ctrl+Alt+G, including prompts not yet loaded in the timeline. Thanks @usrnk1. (#63585)
  • Added the experimental Recent sessions sidebar, with cross-server recency, local pins, reordering, and standard session-row actions.
  • Recent sessions are limited to the last seven days and 30 rows, and can be hidden or muted.
  • Added an experimental customizable sessions sidebar.
  • Mermaid diagrams can now be maximized for zooming and panning. (#59250)
  • Added Alt+Enter to place a follow-up first in the queue and Mod+Enter to steer the next queued prompt.
  • Holding the modifier key now shows tab shortcut numbers.
  • Each server’s terminal shell can be selected from its menu.
  • Added a free-limit panel in place of the composer when applicable.
  • Session IDs are easier to find and copy.

Bug fixes

  • Recent sessions now keep stable recency ordering through list reloads, renames, and completed runs.
  • Fixed browser panes not retrying after server plugins finish loading.
  • Fixed mention suggestions being recreated while typing.
  • Fixed SSH prompts failing to advance or close after connection.
  • Fixed pasted provider-auth redirect URLs being rejected.
  • Restored message footers in the New timeline.
  • Fixed links whose labels contain inline code.
  • Fixed the desktop main process freezing while probing the login-shell environment.
  • Fixed the drag region after changing layout direction.
  • Fixed vertical-tab previews snapping to the wrong tab.
  • Fixed the file-tree sidebar opening animation.
  • Fixed pairing QR-code contrast in light themes. Thanks @ssskay. (#63220)
  • Fixed activity-tab navigation and click handling. Thanks @OpeOginni. (#60168)

SDK

Improvements

  • Event subscriptions can now receive experimental.mcp.event events from MCP event subscriptions.
  • Model capability schemas now advertise supported freeform tool-input formats.
  • Session throughput is now measured from the first streamed token.
  • Session creation documentation now clarifies the parentID field.

Extensions

Improvements

  • Promise-based tools can cooperatively stop through ToolContext.signal.
  • Added the experimental session.fork hook.
  • Promise plugin cleanup failures no longer prevent subsequent plugin replacements.
  • @opencode/plugin/effect now exports Tool, so an execute.before hook can reject a call with Tool.Error without importing @opencode/schema/tool. (#63698)

gastownhall/beads ↗ 2 releases · 2 prerelease
v1.4.0-rc.2 Prerelease2026-11-13T07:49:25Z

beads v1.4.0-rc.2

Pre-compiled binaries for Linux, macOS (Intel & Apple Silicon), Windows (AMD64 & ARM64), Android/Termux (ARM64), and FreeBSD.

Installation (release candidate)

This is a prerelease for validation. The stable install channels — Homebrew, the install.sh / install.ps1 scripts, npm, and PyPI — intentionally keep serving the latest stable release, so they will not install this RC. Use one of the methods below.

Manual download (recommended): Download the archive for your platform from the Assets below, extract it, and place bd on your PATH.

Go install (pinned to this RC):

go install github.com/steveyegge/beads/cmd/bd@v1.4.0-rc.2
# For embedded Dolt support:
CGO_ENABLED=1 GOFLAGS=-tags=gms_pure_go go install github.com/steveyegge/beads/cmd/bd@v1.4.0-rc.2

1.4.0 is a one-way upgrade. Back up first. Consenting to the schema migration (bd migrate schema, --force, or BD_ALLOW_MIGRATE=1) moves a 1.2.x or 1.3.x store to schema v74. After that, bd 1.3.x and older refuse the database; going back means restoring a backup. From 1.4.0-rc.1, rc.2 is a binary swap: it adds no migration.

  • Validate on a copy, or on a store you have backed up with your current bd (bd dolt push, a JSONL export, or bd backup sync).
  • Every Dolt server bd talks to, and the dolt CLI on PATH, must be 2.6.0 or newer.
  • Read Upgrade notes and Known issues under [1.4.0-rc.2] and [1.4.0-rc.1] in CHANGELOG.md before installing.

New in rc.2:

  • bd github sync keeps local label edits again; this was a 1.4.0 regression.
  • Server-mode pulls no longer write the remote URL, token included, into merge commits.
  • Binaries are built with Go 1.27.4 (GO-2026-6859).

Behavior changes to know before installing:

  • bd github now stops when a repository lookup fails. Rerun after a temporary failure. Never clear or repoint another repository's bead links to get past it: the next sync creates unlinked beads as new issues in the configured repository.
  • Server-mode pulls need EXECUTE on dolt_fetch and dolt_merge.
  • bd backup restore refuses a Dolt server on another host. Run it on the server's machine, in a workspace that names the same dolt_database.

Still open:

  • #7283: in server mode, run bare bd migrate once per database if bd doctor reports a version mismatch after bd migrate schema.
  • Go programs that link beads cannot open a proxied-server workspace (#8412, #8323).
  • bd create --repo <url>'s cached clone still writes the URL into merge commits (#8434).

When 1.4.0 ships, install it by hand. bd doctor ranks 1.4.0-rc.N above 1.4.0 (#6595), so it will not tell you an upgrade is available.

Changelog

Bug Fixes

  • 4b6e7f773f48613247374082fe99f17e1fb0f04e fix(backup): refuse a restore a Dolt server on another host would read unchecked (#7192 follow-up) (#8413)
  • 1d6983ac0e4ff418cb58dc4988917794f338dfe7 fix(dolt): server-mode pulls fetch and merge the tracking ref, not DOLT_PULL (bd-e2q8zs) (#8407)
  • bfd3ef3fe23478ba68eee9520f8f1f35752c1ec1 fix(github): #8068 follow-up — fail Init when a repository-name lookup gets no answer (#8399)
  • 22f1221bda7f8c681baf92329387fecfe1e6cd8f fix(tracker): #8004 follow-up — a pull keeps a local label edit while GitHub's labels are unchanged (#8409)

Others

  • beabc87fadfa65514b3e345884944b9c9f88a8fb [release/1.4.0] chore(release): prepare v1.4.0-rc.2 (#8442)
  • d296ce1a64580f2e28252299123c2336857f81c0 build(security): build with Go 1.27.4 for GO-2026-6859 (net/http) (#8431)

v1.4.0-rc.1 Prerelease2026-11-12T21:35:59Z

beads v1.4.0-rc.1

Pre-compiled binaries for Linux, macOS (Intel & Apple Silicon), Windows (AMD64 & ARM64), Android/Termux (ARM64), and FreeBSD.

Installation (release candidate)

This is a prerelease for validation. The stable install channels — Homebrew, the install.sh / install.ps1 scripts, npm, and PyPI — intentionally keep serving the latest stable release, so they will not install this RC. Use one of the methods below.

Manual download (recommended): Download the archive for your platform from the Assets below, extract it, and place bd on your PATH.

Go install (pinned to this RC):

go install github.com/steveyegge/beads/cmd/bd@v1.4.0-rc.1
# For embedded Dolt support:
CGO_ENABLED=1 GOFLAGS=-tags=gms_pure_go go install github.com/steveyegge/beads/cmd/bd@v1.4.0-rc.1

1.4.0 is a one-way upgrade. Back up first. Consenting to the schema migration (bd migrate schema, --force, or BD_ALLOW_MIGRATE=1) moves a 1.2.x or 1.3.x store to schema v74. After that, bd 1.3.x and older refuse the database. There is no going back to 1.3.4 except by restoring a backup.

  • Validate on a copy, or on a store you have backed up with your current bd (bd dolt push, a JSONL export, or bd backup sync).
  • Every Dolt server bd talks to, and the dolt CLI on PATH, must be 2.6.0 or newer.
  • Read Upgrade notes and Known issues under [1.4.0-rc.1] in CHANGELOG.md before installing.

Known issues found after the notes were written (fixes queued for rc.2):

  • bd github sync can lose label edits (#8409, a 1.4.0 regression). A label added or removed locally since the last sync is dropped on both sides, because sync pulls before it pushes. Until rc.2, run bd github push before every bd github sync or pull.
  • bd github can skip beads linked under a renamed or transferred repository (#8399, also in 1.3.3 and 1.3.4). A transient GitHub error during the repository-name lookup is not reported, and a later pull can overwrite those beads' unpushed local edits. Push before pulling, and re-run a sync that hit GitHub errors.
  • bd backup restore --force against a Dolt server with its own filesystem. The rc.1 notes call #8413 open; it is now merged on main for a server on another host and queued for rc.2. A container reached at localhost still needs the backup mounted at the same path.
  • In server mode, bd doctor can report a version mismatch after bd migrate schema (#7283). Run bare bd migrate once per database.

When 1.4.0 ships, install it by hand. bd doctor ranks 1.4.0-rc.N above 1.4.0 (#6595), so it will not tell you an upgrade is available.

Changelog

Features

skill-v4.9.0Skill 4.9.0 Stable2026-11-13T06:18:33Z

  • What a signed-in CLI saves. If you sign in with impeccable login, a full detect of a git repo saves its design health counts and the foundations it found, never your code or file names. Your DESIGN.md and each critique report your agent saves upload in full, and the first upload of each tells you so and names its off command. Setup status reports once a day which coding agents have Impeccable. Each one has its own off switch (impeccable health off, impeccable health design off, impeccable critiques off, impeccable setup off), and forget deletes what was saved. Nothing is saved on CI.

  • Codex's design hook sees shell writes. Codex writes most files through the shell, so the end-of-turn pass used to scan nothing in about half of sessions. It now checks every UI file changed since the session began.

  • A fair shot for every direction. Before dealing directions for a new look, the skill asks only about the product and proposes no look of its own. A product your brief names, like Linear or Stripe, now sets the quality bar instead of fixing the style, so the dealt directions still get a fair look.

  • Audit keeps its report. audit saves its findings next to critique's, and one reports setting turns saving on or off for both.

  • Smaller fixes. A boot note points to /impeccable doctor when the design hook isn't installed. Live mode keeps page fonts in captures and keeps an accepted variant styled on static pages. The engine honors NO_PROXY. Subagents can reach your MCP tools. audit checks right-to-left layouts. Codex's plugin hooks run on Windows, thanks to @MaxFabian25 for the report and @zhangkom for the fix.

different-ai/openwork ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.18.67OpenWork v0.18.67 Stable2026-11-13T06:10:42Z

AI Gateway capabilities are now available to every organization

  • More AI Gateway features are available to every organization. Auth & Security, config import and export, AI Gateway API keys for other tools, custom OpenAI-compatible providers, and Smart Routers are now available to every organization, including self-hosted deployments; their completed-rollout switches are gone. TrueFoundry is also available directly in the provider picker. Organizations that had not enabled Auth & Security now have 30-day session limits and admins confirm their identity after 15 minutes for sensitive changes. Claude prompt caching also lowers the cost of long sessions through AI Gateway.
  • Admins have clearer controls for roles and computers. Admins can assign built-in or custom roles to people or teams, and members can disconnect signed-in computers and set what other devices may do on each one. New sandbox organizations make joiners super admins by default. The My Sessions pages have been removed from Den, and a member's Workbot cloud computers are deleted when they leave an organization.
  • Workbot and remote tasks recover more cleanly. Workbot can hand over apps as cards, recovered tasks no longer show as failed, and remote tasks can bring questions and computer-use approvals back to the chat that started them. Unresponsive cloud computers can be replaced, and idle ones pause again.
  • Desktop chats and setup are more dependable. File actions find files inside project subfolders, OpenCode v2 web search cards show their results, OpenWork Models no longer appear missing from the model catalog, and remotely launched chats keep their model choice. Claude Code sign-in can continue in your browser, and desktop Automations run with models that have no variant.
  • Workbot Documents can play audio and video. Admins no longer configure a Workbot step limit or separate mail and chat sources; access follows the Google Workspace, Microsoft 365, or Slack connector they allow. OpenWork-built interactive views are now called Artifacts throughout chat, the library, and sharing.
  • Website visitors and admins have new setup guidance. The website adds an EU sovereignty guide and checklist, and the docs add a knowledge-work cost benchmark and an OIDC sign-in guide. Connector setup also shows the callback URL needed by OAuth providers.

Full changelog: https://openworklabs.com/docs/changelog · Compare

Windows installers are signed using Microsoft Artifact Signing.

heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.9.41 Stable2026-11-13T04:57:14Z

HyperFrames v0.9.41

Released on 2026-11-13.

Fast capture works again on Chrome 157 and newer, which fixes Windows exports that failed at drawElementImage. Local renders keep Chrome's sandbox on where the host supports it and launch Chrome with only the environment it needs and explain a failed launch with the environment it had, cloud render --json lists the files an upload leaves out, publish copies only media, font, CSS and JS files from outside the project and leaves out the rest that came in with an import, a video variable given as a link now plays its sound once, media ids that differ only by case get separate folders so a render no longer fails with a missing file, and the Studio timeline and overlays read an opaque preview over its channel.

Features

  • CLI: Cloud render --json lists the files an upload leaves out (2591abfbf, #7631).
  • Studio: Read overlay geometry from an opaque preview over its channel (faff4e16a, #7628).
  • Studio: Timeline select and selection refresh read an opaque preview over its channel (aed4c18fe, #7613).

Fixes

  • CLI: Copy only media, font, CSS and JS files from outside the project (89a7aa300, #7636).
  • Engine: Keep media ids that differ only by case in separate folders (b54e4981f, #7635).
  • Producer: A video variable given as a link plays its sound once (2760fa119, #7633).
  • Engine: Diagnose a failed Chrome launch with the env it had (9701f4bbf, #7634).
  • Engine: Launch Chrome with only the env it needs (24d56e681, #7629).
  • Engine: Diagnose the launch attempt whose error surfaces (43722355a, #7630).
  • CLI: Publish leaves out outside files that came in with an imported project (a810a8b00, #7598).
  • Engine: Keep Chrome's sandbox on for local renders where the host supports it (400a4e228, #7625).
  • Engine: Fast capture works on Chrome 157+, which wants content=drawable (9c3258ccb, #7626).
  • Producer,docs: Motion blur's compile hint and preview claim say what is measured (e09fe6e50, #7581).

Internal

  • Tests: Move gen-os-static asset links to static.heygen.ai (aadd5bd6f, #7536).
  • Give Test (runtime) 15 minutes (d30f1f4ce, #7627).
  • Engine: The blur trail mask covers the shutter's sweep, not only whole frames (2df837be9, #7624).

HyperFrames v0.9.40

Released on 2026-11-12.

open-gsd/gsd-core ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.21.0 Stable2026-11-13T04:05:43Z

Install

npm i @opengsd/gsd-core@1.21.0
# or
npm i @opengsd/gsd-core@latest

What's Changed

Added

  • The Community Capability Registry now lists Beads Lifecycle — discover the independently maintained davdittrich/gsd-beads capability, which syncs plan tasks into Beads (bd) issues, closes them after each wave, and blocks /gsd-ship while blocking_open or diverged is not 0 or BEADS.md is missing (its ship:pre gate also blocks while bd is missing, unless beads.ship_gate is false), with exact install and uninstall commands. (#5307) (#5384)
  • The Community Capability Registry now lists musk — discover the independently maintained bitcreed/gsd-musk-simplification capability, a subtraction pass that challenges milestones, requirements and implementation before they are built, logs every cut reversibly, and prices the savings from the project's own token usage, with exact install and uninstall commands. (#5869) (#5872)
  • The Community Capability Registry now lists Standing Orders — discover the independently maintained The-Artificer-of-Ciphers-LLC/standing-orders capability, which holds project-wide business rules in one ORDERS.yaml, gives the planner only the rules that apply to the phase, blocks planning on rule conflicts or edits to a rule's check files, requires GSD's fail-first prover to prove each applicable test rule after every execution wave, and records at ship which rules the phase was held to, with exact install and uninstall commands. (#6000) (#6004)
  • verify plan-structure --replay checks that recorded baselines were measured — a plan's [HEAD=<value> EXPECT…] baseline was a claim nobody checked until the executor ran the phase. The opt-in --replay flag runs each criterion's grep or read-only git command as direct argv with no shell, and reports where the printed value differs from the recorded one. It never changes the exit code. (#4310) (#6057)
  • Plans can record a checkable baseline for every count/match gate — write [HEAD=<value> EXPECT<op><value>] beside a criterion and verify plan-structure rejects one that already holds at HEAD (a gate that passes whether or not the work happens), warns on the old free-form # baseline at HEAD: comment, and reports unreadable notations without changing the exit code. (#4309) (#6006)
  • Projects can exempt a committed env template from the secret-read guard — a repo whose placeholder template is named like a secret (.env.production) could not let agents read it without removing the guard for every .env file; list it in hooks.secret_read_exempt and the guard lets it through while it stays committed as a regular file, and an unconfigured project blocks exactly as before. (#4432) (#5834)
  • gsd-tools query project-context prints the resolved project context — one read-only verb now shows which checkout GSD acts in, which directory owns .planning/, the main checkout, and a provenance record (source, reason, degraded, warnings), so a wrong-checkout symptom in a worktree can be diagnosed instead of guessed. (#6043) (#6071)

Changed

  • gsd-tools commit refuses to write outside the checkout it runs in — a commit from a worktree now exits non-zero with committed: false and reason: "outside_worktree" when the working directory, a declared path, or the planning project it would write to lies outside that worktree, including a worktree inside a planning.sub_repos workspace whose .planning/ belongs to the workspace; outside a repository it refuses with not_git_repo. Pass --project-dir <checkout> to commit in the checkout that owns the path. (#6043) (#6071)
  • The project-root walk stops at a symlinked $HOME in either spelling — with $HOME a symlink, a cwd spelled through its target (what process.cwd() reports), or the reverse, walked past $HOME and could take a .planning/ above it as the project; a cwd at $HOME itself with no .planning/ walked above it with any spelling. Both now stop at $HOME, and the isolation hooks, which resolve through the same walk, agree. (#5624) (#5847)
  • gsd-tools commands reject stray words and unknown flags they used to ignore — a misspelled or unsupported flag, a missing required input, or an extra word now stops the command with a usage error naming the problem, instead of being silently ignored; a repeated flag now uses its first value on every command (resolve-execution, update-context, loop render-hooks and record-dispatch-isolation took the last one, or preferred the = form, before); every command's accepted flags are listed in docs/CLI-SURFACE-MANIFEST.json (#6087). (#6100)
  • /gsd-autonomous stops asking about reversible technical choices and asks preference questions without a recommendation — smart discuss now decides cheap-to-undo technical questions (file layout, naming, internal data shapes) itself and lists them in a "Decided autonomously" block, CONTEXT.md and DISCUSSION-LOG.md; hard-to-reverse questions are still asked with a recommendation, and product/UX questions are asked neutrally. (#5069) (#6069)
  • The Claude orchestration Workflow backend now checks whether workflows are actually turned on — detection used to gate on the installed Agent SDK version, which says nothing about a Claude Code session, and missed an organization or user that had disabled workflows. It now honors CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_WORKFLOWS and disableWorkflows / enableWorkflows in managed, project and user settings, refuses to emit a script past Claude Code's 4,096-per-call and 1,000-agent caps, and gets each worktree's metadata back as validated structured output. (#4747) (#6070)

Deprecated

  • claude_orchestration.min_agent_sdk_version is deprecated and ignored — the Workflow backend no longer gates on the Agent SDK version, so the key, the --agent-sdk-version flag and GSD_AGENT_SDK_VERSION have no effect; existing configs still load, and the key can be removed. (#4747) (#6070)

Fixed

  • A refused install no longer leaves GSD half-upgraded — when a runtime's install destination is an untrusted symlink, that runtime's install now refuses before writing anything, so its previous install stays intact. In a command that installs several runtimes, runtimes installed before the refusal stay installed. Set GSD_ALLOW_SYMLINKED_DEST=1 to install through the symlink. (#5179) (#5828)
  • Catch-up merges no longer pull the base branch into a phase's evaluation scope — a merge commit listed as a task commit reported every file the base branch changed as the phase's own work, so review, UI safety, schema drift and decision-coverage gates evaluated the wrong files; a merge now contributes only its conflict resolutions and edits, the base branch's files no longer show up as unlisted phase work, and unlisted phase work (including on a sub-branch merged in, or an edit to a file the base also touched) stays named in the outside-the-union list, and a SUMMARY whose every listed commit is a merge degrades to the phase range instead of reporting the merge's edits as the whole phase. (#5751) (#5973)
  • phase add, phase add-batch and phase insert refuse a STATE milestone with no ROADMAP heading, or one already shipped — when STATE.md named a milestone (such as v9.9) that ROADMAP.md has no heading for, or one ROADMAP.md marks shipped, these verbs numbered the new phase and wrote its Depends on line against the wrong milestones (every unshipped one, or the closed one) and reported success; they now stop before writing anything and name the milestone, quoted and length-capped. A version mentioned in a Phase title, a code fence or an HTML comment no longer counts as a milestone heading. A check mark emoji that appears only inside a parenthetical naming another version (such as v2.1 Next (after v2.0 prep) carrying one) no longer marks the milestone shipped; every other closed-status word or check mark still does. A milestone whose name contains a status word, such as "Failed Payment Recovery" or "Closed Beta", is still read as shipped, so the refusal tells you to rename it or add an active marker such as the construction emoji or "(in progress)". (#5726) (#6003)
  • The fork-probe cache stays inside the project and out of git — the cache no longer writes through a symlinked .gsd that points outside the project, and a fresh project no longer shows .gsd/ as untracked or sweeps it into git add -A. (#5757) (#5908)
  • Installs into a nested or unusually named config directory keep correct paths on every runtime — the installer rewrote ~/.claude references in several passes, and the converters that run afterwards (agents, the gsd-core/ spec tree, OpenCode and Kilo commands and skills, command bodies, the Codex agent TOML) scanned the emitted prefix again, so a --config-dir such as ~/.claude/profiles/work, ~/.claude.d or ~/.copilot/work was rewritten inside its own output, and $& in a config dir was expanded into the text around it. A Kilo install wrote paths like .../profiles/work/profiles/work/gsd-core, an OpenCode install wrote paths like .claude/~/.config/opencode/x, and a Codex agent TOML got ~/.codex in place of $&. Each reference is now rewritten once and the emitted prefix is shielded from every later converter, for Kilo, OpenCode, Codex, Cline, CodeBuddy, Hermes, Qwen, Windsurf, Kimi, Trae agents and spec tree, Augment commands, and the Copilot and Antigravity @ includes for a config dir under their own default home. Intentional changes to default-install output: (1) Windsurf global installs turned ~/.devin/skills/x and $HOME/.devin/skills/x into ~/$HOME/.codeium/windsurf/skills/x and $HOME/$HOME/...; they now give $HOME/.codeium/windsurf/skills/x. (2) Claude Code's own files such as ~/.claude.json, $HOME/.claude.json, ~/.claude-plugin and ./.claude-plugin were rewritten into nonexistent names like $HOME/.cline.json; they now stay unchanged. (3) A Trae local install wrote $HOME/.trae/... and ~/.trae paths, including @$HOME/.trae/gsd-core/... includes, into its skills, which point at the global directory; they now point at the project's .trae/. A comparison of real default installs of all 18 installable runtimes, global and local, against the previous build found no other difference in the installed files. (#5715) (#5906)
  • execute-phase no longer marks a finished phase as executing — a resume that dispatched no plan (the verify-only resume, a filter that selects nothing, or a phase stuck on blocked or not-ready plans) still rewrote STATE.md's Current Position, Status and Last activity to "Executing, Plan 1 of N"; the phase start is now recorded only once a plan is about to run (#5843). (#6026)
  • Commit gates no longer halt in planning.sub_repos workspaces — check evaluation-scope returned unresolvable (exit 69) whenever the workspace root was not a git repo, so the safe-resume gate, the TDD gate and the executor commit spot-check stopped /gsd-execute-phase. The resolver now reads each configured sub-repo and merges the commits newest first. (#5867) (#6085)
  • effort sync --apply no longer makes the next install back up synced agents as local patches — the sync rewrote agent files without updating gsd-file-manifest.json, so the next install treated every synced agent as a local modification; the manifest now records the synced files. (#5683) (#5899)
  • config-get --default now prints the JSON value it spells — --default '[]' printed the string "[]" instead of an array, so /gsd-code-review stopped before spawning its reviewer in any project that never set workflow.code_review_depth_overrides; the same applied to numbers, booleans, objects and null. --raw --default null on a secret key now shows (unset) instead of ****, and planner.stall_detection_enabled --default false now returns false. config-set and config-get --default no longer turn a blank value or hex text into a number: ' ', ' 5' and 0x10 are kept as text. (#6029) (#6077)
  • Planners no longer put symbols or endpoints in key-link file fields that fail verify key-links — The shared planner guidance now states that from: and to: must be project-relative file paths, with symbols and endpoints in via:. The planner's Step 5 pointer names that contract and loads this guidance while authoring key links (#4946). (#5149)
  • Ghost REQ-ID warning no longer fires on registered requirements — phase complete warned "not registered anywhere" for a requirement whose checkbox used a mark other than space or x (such as [~]) or whose Traceability table has no Status column, and requirements mark-complete reported the same row as table_unmatched; both now see the row, and a row in a table without a Status column gets its own write-skipped message. (#5844) (#6025)
  • GitHub Release pages list the release's CHANGELOG entries — a hotfix's release page had an empty What's Changed because GitHub built it from merged PRs, which a hotfix's cherry-picks are not; every stable release page now shows that release's CHANGELOG section instead of GitHub's PR list. (#5922) (#5930)
  • A named-pipe SUMMARY file no longer hangs phase commands — a FIFO or endless-source SUMMARY in a phase directory made phase lookup, phase-plan-index and the execute-phase and plan-phase init commands block forever; SUMMARY status is now read through one non-blocking, regular-file-only, size-capped reader. (#5789) (#5979)
  • A named pipe, link or oversized file at the dispatch isolation record can no longer hang or mislead subagent dispatch — such a record could block every subagent dispatch indefinitely or be trusted from outside the project; it is now treated as absent. (#5758) (#5927)
  • Code-review fix now runs whenever code review is enabled — /gsd-code-review-fix and /gsd-code-review --fix no longer exit with "code-review capability inactive" when workflow.code_review_point is execute:wave:post; the gate follows the workflow.code_review toggle. (#5753) (#5929)
  • Inserted phases no longer carry "(INSERTED)" into their name — roadmap get-phase and every init command's phase_name kept the marker phase insert writes into the heading, so execute-phase wrote it into STATE.md's current phase name and the Phase line could not be read back; the name is now bare everywhere, matching roadmap analyze (#5824). (#6023)
  • init --validate now warns on a plan-count mismatch for Plan: N of M and Current Plan: N of M — STATE.md written in the compound or hybrid shape recorded its plan total only in the position line, so init execute-phase and init plan-phase reported state_warnings: [] even when the disk plan count disagreed. (#5786) (#5975)
  • Two orchestrators in one checkout no longer deny each other's sequential dispatches — when parallel workstreams ran execute-phase in the same checkout, each recorded isolation decision overwrote the other's, so a plan resolved to run without a worktree was refused by the isolation guard; each dispatch's decision is now kept for its own phase and plan. (#5682) (#5891)
  • state prune no longer loses entries when a write fails — it now writes STATE-ARCHIVE.md first, atomically and under the STATE.md lock, and puts both files back if the STATE.md write fails; re-running a prune that was interrupted between the two writes finishes it without archiving its entries twice; a symlinked or special-file STATE-ARCHIVE.md is refused instead of written through, a named pipe at STATE.md no longer hangs state commands, and a failed auto-prune in phase complete is reported as a warning (#5831). (#5981)
  • drift-guard phase-status and workstream progress read milestone-prefixed phases correctly — under phase_id_convention: milestone-prefixed every M-NN phase named in ROADMAP Progress rows or STATE.md read as the bare milestone, so phase-status compared STATE against the milestone's first phase and reported a false drift, and workstream progress collapsed the milestone's phases into one. (#5675) (#5887)
  • phase insert names the milestone when the phase belongs to another one — inserting after a phase that lives in a different milestone than the current one reported Phase N not found in ROADMAP.md, the same text as a phase missing from the file; the error now names the milestone that holds it and the current one. (#5679) (#5893)
  • new-project and new-milestone point to the right next step — the closing hand-off said /gsd-plan-phase starts execution; it now recommends /gsd-discuss-phase, with /gsd-plan-phase to plan directly and /gsd-execute-phase to execute. (#5723) (#5905)
  • Gates now accept phase directories under a symlinked out-of-repo .planning — check decision-coverage-plan, decision-coverage-verify, gap-analysis-plan-post, evaluation-scope and predicate --phase-dir no longer fail with "path escapes its allowed directory", and check verify-command-paths --dir no longer reports its unresolvable "--dir resolves outside the project root" result, for a phase directory under the project's own .planning; all six resolve their path argument through the shared containment helper, and paths outside the project and its own .planning are still refused. check tdd-red-evidence keeps its own single-root check and is not changed (#5474). (#5510)
  • A linked worktree of a planning.sub_repos workspace now resolves the workspace's .planning/ — inside such a worktree GSD fell back to the repository's main checkout and read and wrote the wrong planning store; it now resolves the workspace's .planning/. (#4908) (#6071)
  • A config file that is a named pipe or device no longer hangs gsd-tools — a FIFO at .planning/config.json or a workstream config.json blocked every command, hook, plugin and installer step that reads configuration; each read is now bounded, the command continues without that file (a workstream falls back to the root config), a config.json over 1 MiB is refused, and temporary files are created exclusively without following links. A write that cannot be published atomically now refuses a device, named-pipe, symlinked, multiply-hard-linked or write-only (0200) target instead of writing to it (the atomic publish replaces a link or extra hard link entry and never writes through it, and it refuses to publish if the parent directory was swapped meanwhile) (a user-chosen --output device or pipe such as /dev/stdout is still written, through the descriptor it names rather than by reopening the path, waiting without a time limit for a slow reader), and an existing file keeps its permission bits after a write. (#5759) (#5932)
  • A test file that crashed at load can no longer pass as RED evidence — check tdd-red-evidence returned RED_EVIDENCE_OK when the target was the crashing file's own entry (a vitest bad.test.js, a jest-junit Test suite failed to run, or a node:test file-level failure); it now returns INVALID_RED (fixture_or_load_failure). (#5721) (#5917)
  • phase complete, state sync and init progress read milestone-prefixed phase ids correctly — under phase_id_convention: milestone-prefixed, re-completing a phase such as 3-01 while STATE.md was at 3-02 rewound STATE to the completed phase, a first-time completion advanced STATE to phase 3 named "02: beta", and roadmap checklist rows, phase headings and phase directories such as 03-02-beta were not recognised by init progress, init manager (its checked-phase fallback), find-phase (which reported phase 03 named "02-beta") and the planning snapshot. (#5676) (#5896)
  • Verifier no longer drops pre-verified human checks — an auto task's <human-check> whose <expected> already recorded a verdict could be omitted from the end-of-phase human verification list; every such block is now harvested with its expected text unchanged. (#5741) (#5928)
  • Capability plan:pre steps and gates now run when planning a phase — a plan:pre step that runs a command, a skill step outside auto-chain mode (such as mempalace recall), and any gate other than the built-in UI and drift checks were listed by loop render-hooks plan:pre and then silently skipped, so a capability's blocking gate could not stop planning. Plan-phase now dispatches every active plan:pre step and evaluates every active gate before the planner starts, in manual and auto-chain mode. Planning a phase whose research already existed, was skipped or had just completed also no longer jumps past the validation-strategy step and the security, UI and codebase-drift checks. (#5982) (#5983)
  • Windows hooks no longer block every Read, Grep and Bash call when Git Bash is not found — Claude Code runs hooks in PowerShell when it cannot find Git Bash, where the installed bash node-runner could not parse and the secret-read guard blocked every tool call; on Windows the installer now always writes a PowerShell runner with an explicit shell: "powershell" (Git Bash or not, portable installs included) and rewrites existing bash-runner entries, including the statusline, on reinstall. (#5850) (#6067)
  • The secret-read guard reads past sudo's -a/-c and doas's -a values again — sudo -c ls cat .env, sudo --auth-type echo cat .env and doas -a ls cat .env are denied again, as they were in 1.18.0; the wrapper table now lists those value-taking options from sudo(8) and doas(1). (#5852) (#5874)
  • Commit and branch guards now recognise git --no-advice, --config-env and --attr-source — a git commit or git checkout -b written with one of these global options before the subcommand was classified as not-a-commit and slipped past the commit-message and workflow guards. (#5821) (#6001)
  • Copilot and Codex agents keep their full tool list — agents that declare tools: as a YAML block list (gsd-security-auditor, gsd-nyquist-auditor) were installed with a single mangled entry such as ['- read'] for Copilot or - Read for Codex instead of every declared tool. (#5790) (#5974)
  • The planner now reads MemPalace recall when planning a phase — with the mempalace capability enabled, the MEMORY-RECALL.md file the plan:pre recall step writes into the phase directory was never named in the planner's required reading, so prior decisions, patterns and surprises recalled from the palace never reached planning. The planner is now told to read it whenever it exists; phases without it plan as before. (#5960) (#5994)
  • Human-recorded UAT results are no longer lost when execute-phase re-runs — a phase that already had a filled UAT file had it reset to all-pending and committed when verification again needed human checks; new items are now appended and existing results kept. (#5845) (#6081)

Full Changelog: https://github.com/open-gsd/gsd-core/compare/v1.20.1...v1.21.0

stablyai/orca ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.2581.4.258 Stable2026-11-13T01:35:38Z

Previous release: 1.4.257 — Smoother long chats, saved browser feedback and better dictation.

Smoother workspaces, restored phone scrolling & Copilot Chat ✨

Orca 1.4.258 cuts pauses when opening large workspace collections, moving windows and changing the layout. Local usage updates do less work on the process handling your keystrokes, helping Chat UI and terminal users keep working while usage refreshes.

  • 📱 Scroll phone chats again: On chat pages served by the desktop, swipe through messages and sideways through wide code blocks. Pinch with two fingers to resize chat text without zooming the whole page.
  • 💬 Use GitHub Copilot in Chat: Choose Launch chat to stream replies, inspect tool output and review file diffs in a conversation you can reopen after restarting Orca.
  • ⌨️ Start Pi with your chosen model and thinking level: The terminal launcher applies your model choice and offers reasoning effort before you send the first prompt.
  • 🗂️ Add projects with fewer steps: Browse a folder, clone a URL or create a project directly from Add project. Switch machines inside the menu; from New worktree, it starts with your selected Run on host.

Highlights

  • Keep conversations moving: Usage-limit Hand Off to Another Agent includes the full saved conversation. Generated images stay beside the final answer, and closing a window with a replying chat uses the running-work confirmation. Answer worker permission prompts from the coordinator, with pending requests above the composer and receipts in conversation order.
  • Browse, review and open files: Extensions are enabled by default in Settings → Browser. Git Graph reduces repeated work while scrolling and paging; idle editors stop polling copy hints. Unsupported document tabs offer Open with default app, downloading a copy first for SSH or paired-host files.
  • Switch Claude accounts remotely: Under Ask, a phone or CLI switch resumes terminal sessions on the selected account without a desktop dialog. Busy sessions finish their turn first; Never restart still keeps them as they are.
  • Read clearer phone code and history: Phone pages gain syntax colours, pinned diff gutters and PR-state icons. Session History finds compressed Codex archives, OMP profiles and larger OpenCode 2 sessions.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • Copilot Chat: Requires Copilot CLI 1.0.88+ and sign-in. Models appear after starting; plans without a model choice use Auto. Session History import into Chat is not supported yet. Avoid switching a chat’s session to allow-all in an external terminal: that permission setting persists when reopened, even if Orca shows Ask.
  • Phone updates: The desktop supplies updated phone web pages; it does not replace the installed native app. New native iOS builds from this source require iOS 16.4+. If a tab says Update mobile, update the phone app and reconnect.

Nightly release from commit: 0ab67dc60a4c013f94ccb96dfe08dd6230e7f185

npm packages, rust crates and python wheels are available under this version 0.11.1-nightly.0ab67dc

Neovim Plugin

  • {target}.so / .dylib / .dll - Lua module for Neovim

C FFI Library (for Bun/Node/Python)

  • c-lib-{target}.so / .dylib / .dll - C FFI library

MCP Server

  • fff-mcp-{target} - MCP server binary

Python Package

  • python/*.whl / python/*.tar.gz - Python wheels and sdist
