can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v18.20.0 Stable2026-11-12T23:40:30Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core

Changed

  • prepareCompaction() runs as the compaction.prepare loop phase, so a ui.loop-blocked stall from a cold token walk over a long branch names compaction instead of "unknown" (#15027, #18867 by @wolfiesch)

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Added

  • Added the google-decisions judgment API for Google's Gemini Decisions API (gemini-decision-1), with text and image input (#19060 by @kevcube)
  • Added apiKey to successful OAuthAccessResolution results from oauth.accessById() and oauth.accessAll(): the key normal requests send for that account, which differs from accessToken for providers with structured credentials (#19030 by @Xytronix)
  • Added an optional exemptionAlias to ToolCallLoopGuard so transport calls can match the dispatched tool's name in exemptTools (#18860)
  • Added the liquid-systemone judgment API for Liquid AI's decision models, which rejects image sets over Liquid's 10,000 32×32-patch request cap before sending (#18076, #18100 by @SuperUserA)
  • Added the nace-systemone judgment API for Nace's Drex decision models, which sends images as Drex's top-level images data URLs and replaces the base64 image, audio, video and PDF data URLs that Drex rejects in judged text with a description (#18950 by @alphastorm)

Fixed

  • Fixed a spent Codex account retrying every minute for days when its siblings were only refused the requested model: their short model-policy blocks no longer count as a sibling becoming available, so the usage-limit wait follows the spent account's reset and model fallback can take over (#17637, #19019 by @wolfiesch)
  • Auth-gateway client tokens (serve --clients) can no longer set who a request is from, who pays, or how the gateway reaches the provider: chat routes drop their request headers, userProfileId, initiatorOverride, metadata, serviceTier, cacheRetention and the other gateway-only options, while the gateway token keeps them (#19067 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed the auth gateway's /v1/pi/stream dropping taskBudget, include and anthropicPrefixMismatchBehavior, which its other chat routes accept (#19067 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed requests to Vertex AI Mistral partner models (mistral-medium-3, mistral-small-2503, codestral-2) failing because they were sent in Anthropic's request format (#19010, #19022 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • Added Gemini's xhigh thinking level, sent on the Gemini API and Vertex for models whose ladder exposes it (Gemini 4 Hydrogen).
  • Fixed omp failing to start with ENOTDIR: not a directory, stat '~/.aws/credentials' when ~/.aws is a file instead of a directory (#19083 by @iamshatter)

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added the Gemini Decision 1 judge model (google/gemini-decision-1, GEMINI_API_KEY); assign it to the judge role to use it (#19060 by @kevcube)
  • Added Nace (nace) with the drex-max-v1.1, drex-max-finance and drex-v2.0 judge models and /login nace (NACE_API_KEY) (#18950 by @alphastorm)
  • Added Gemini 4 Hydrogen (gemini-4-hydrogen) on Google, Vertex, OpenCode Zen, OpenRouter, Vercel AI Gateway, and Kilo, with low, medium, high, and xhigh thinking levels on the native Google transports.

Changed

  • Factory Droid requests identify as droid 1.7.1 and report the interactive CLI as their surface, and prefix-bound Claude models (Opus, Sonnet and Haiku 5.5, Fable 5.1) on Factory's direct Anthropic route now ask Anthropic to drop stale signed thinking instead of rejecting a turn whose earlier context changed (#18771 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • Fixed alibaba-token-plan/qwen4-max-preview (including its qwen4.0-max-preview alias) showing no context or output limits, image input, or thinking levels (#18986).
  • Fixed Vertex AI Mistral partner models (mistral-medium-3, mistral-small-2503, codestral-2) misrouting to the first-party Gemini endpoint; they now ride the publishers/mistralai raw-predict endpoint with ADC auth and an OpenAI-shaped body (#19010, #19022 by @yuzu-octopus).
  • Added Liquid AI (liquid) with the d1.1 and d1-ultra judge models and /login liquid (LIQUID_API_KEY) (#18076, #18100 by @SuperUserA)

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Breaking Changes

  • secrets.yml regex rules are now Rust regex patterns run natively, in linear time: lookarounds and backreferences are no longer supported, and a (?P<secret>…) group marks the part to redact (text around it is context that stays); \d, \w and \s are Unicode-aware. A regex rule that does not compile stops startup with an error naming the file and entry instead of being skipped. Migrate (?<=password=)\S+ to password=(?P<secret>\S+); see docs/secrets.md (#18965 by @H4vC)
  • Secret placeholders are now four camelCased words after the friendly name, e.g. GitHubToken_correctHorseStapleBattery, with no $$ delimiters, and the transcript shows the real value wherever the model wrote one. Placeholders in the old $$NAME_HASH:CASE$$ form, in sessions saved before this change, stay literal text, including in tool calls the model copies them into; start a new session to get working placeholders (#19014 by @H4vC)
  • What the model writes from placeholders (replies, thinking, tool calls, summaries) is stored as written and replayed byte-identical, keeping thinking signatures and prompt caches valid; model output that still holds a value (written while redaction was off, or before the secret was configured) is redacted on replay. Values a regex rule finds later no longer rewrite earlier history. A friendlyName is shown to the model as written and is dropped only when it spells out its own secret (#19014 by @H4vC)
  • Nix builds with withAppleFm = false no longer compile the Apple Foundation Models bridge with the host's Xcode (#18911)
  • Apple Silicon Nix builds include Apple Foundation Models support instead of silently building without it (#16584 by @honsunrise)
  • ask over RPC hosts without a form dialog now shows each prompt with the form's surrounding paragraphs, lists, preformatted blocks and tables instead of only the question (#18927)

Added

  • Added the Gemini Decision 1 judge (google/gemini-decision-1) for the judge role, and models.yml accepts the google-decisions API (#19060 by @kevcube)
  • ssh:// host listing, ssh:// autocomplete and /ssh list now include the Host aliases from ~/.ssh/config and /etc/ssh/ssh_config (following Include); ssh.json entries win on a name clash
  • Added find.verifyModel, a model selector whose judge answers only find's passage verification while filename and sketch ranking stay on the judge role (#19006 by @alphastorm)
  • Added omp bench --account to benchmark a specific stored OAuth account, and report rejected priority requests instead of silently benchmarking standard speed.
  • Text a browser or computer call types into a page or app, such as a password, no longer appears in plain text in an eval cell's status lines or the session file (#18981 by @will-bogusz)
  • Browser page-script observations in Tern and WebKit now mask password values, including values used as accessible names or descriptions (#18983 by @will-bogusz)
  • Chromium main-world waitForFunction calls no longer expose Puppeteer's internal ARIA query bindings to page scripts (#18883 by @will-bogusz)
  • On macOS a computer run that ends while secure input is on now starts with a note naming the app holding it and saying the user's Escape and typing go unseen until it ends, instead of nothing while capabilities() still reported globalEscape: true (#18982 by @will-bogusz)
  • Windows computer runs now explain when a locked or disconnected session, or a secure desktop, prevents capture and input from confirming your app's state (#18947 by @will-bogusz)
  • Computer helpers now reject unknown option and filter keys instead of silently ignoring them, including keys unused by the selected wait or element press overload, and name valid keys and likely spelling corrections (#18753 by @will-bogusz)
  • Selector waits on Chromium-backed browser tabs and their frames now accept state: "attached" | "detached" | "visible" | "hidden" alongside Puppeteer's visible/hidden forms, including live snapshot refs and waits that span a navigation, and reject unknown options, invalid values or conflicting visibility flags instead of silently ignoring them. Frame wait timeouts remain catchable later in the same run; Tern and cmux tabs are unchanged (#18924 by @will-bogusz)
  • Chromium main-world waitForFunction calls rearm across navigation after an earlier wait instead of rejecting with "Waiting failed" (#18884 by @will-bogusz)
  • In Chromium browser tabs, tab.hover and element-handle hover() on a hidden match now wait for it to appear and, if it never does, fail with the reason (and a selector's match count), as clicks do, instead of failing at once with Puppeteer's "Node is either not clickable or not an Element" (#18750 by @will-bogusz)
  • Treating the result of browser tab.console() or tab.errors() as an array now fails with a message saying to read its .entries, instead of slice is not a function, and the browser docs state the { entries, nextSeq, dropped } shape (#18738 by @will-bogusz)
  • A printed browser tab.observe() result, including a full observation returned through tab.run() (not arbitrary objects with an elements array), or one left as a JavaScript Eval cell's last value, shows every element, one line each, instead of the first 100 followed by ... N more items; an observation changed after it returned, including in-place prototype edits, prints as plain inspect output, every element included (#18772 by @will-bogusz)

Changed

  • Secret redaction no longer rescans the whole conversation on every request: text already redacted in an earlier request is reused until a new secret changes the outcome, and secrets.yml regex rules skip text missing their leading literal, cutting redaction on a large session from 17–55 ms to about 1 ms per request and the first request after loading by 2–4×
  • Trimmed the default system prompt: the tool-choice rules now only point to read, edit and find; the reasoning-format, AST and subagent-limit guidance is gone (ast_edit/ast_grep state their preference in their own summaries); the workflow, delivery and critical rules are shorter and no longer repeat each other; Spotlight's note on how tool results are shaped is a short list under Tool I/O instead of a paragraph at the end; and xd:// devices are listed by bare name
  • edit.modelVariants now picks the longest matching model pattern instead of the first, so a specific key like openai/gpt-5 wins over gpt whatever the config order (#17835 by @DeprecatedLuke)
  • Browser tabs open in omp's own headless WebKit engine instead of managed Chromium when the engine, ABKit, is installed (~/.o2/lib/abkit/ABHost, ABHost.exe on Windows, the location Tern uses too; or the browser.webkitPath setting / PI_BROWSER_WEBKIT_PATH): pages see a visible, focused window with no automation flag, and a default tab runs no omp script in the page (console, errors and the request log come from the engine; route, geolocation/locale/offline emulation, vitals and reactEnable add page code only when called; response bodies are unavailable there). Chromium still opens headed tabs where the engine ships no viewer and takes over, saying why, when the engine cannot start; app: "chromium" picks Chromium for one open and app: "webkit" requires WebKit (with headed: true its tab opens in a window), and the browser.webkit setting or PI_BROWSER_WEBKIT=0 turns it off. On macOS the engine has WebGL, WebGPU, silent video and audio playback, Web Audio, WebRTC, WebAuthn, gamepads and Web Share, and its TLS handshake, HTTP/2 settings and request headers are the ones Safari sends
  • omp downloads its WebKit engine, ABKit, from build.stencil.so into ~/.o2/lib/abkit (checked against the build's SHA-256) for app: "webkit" when it is missing, and before Chrome for Testing when an open finds no browser installed; a failed download leaves that open on Chromium and says why
  • The task tool description now lists only the delivery rules (results auto-deliver, wait, unverified completed); the read proc://, write proc://<id>/kill, job-expiry and write agent:// reference arrives with the session's first spawn result
  • The ask.timeout setting, README and RPC schema docs now describe the ask tool as an HTML form that submits its current values on timeout, not a question picker that auto-selects an option
  • The bash tool description lists the embedded shell's in-process builtins again, straight from the shell, so it follows every PI_DISABLE_* switch and the platform
  • read's selectors for SQLite, archives, JSON queries, executables, video and SVG, and the sub-path grammar of proc://, ssh:// and issue:///pr://, no longer ship with every request: the model reads them on demand at xd://read/sources and xd://read/urls, and they stay inline when read is off
  • /browser visible and /browser headless now show and hide the windows of omp's WebKit tabs at once instead of waiting for the next open: where the engine ships its viewer (macOS), each tab gets a window of its own that the user can also click, scroll and type in, and headed: true or browser.headless off keeps a tab on WebKit with a window instead of sending it to Chromium. Chromium tabs still change mode with the next open
  • In a Tern whose engine reads the DOM natively, browser observe, selectors, clicks, typing, element reads and waits no longer run script in the page: the engine finds elements (across frames and closed shadow roots) and every action is trusted input at the point it resolved; type then types at the caret its click places, and text/ selectors ignore case
  • A browser screenshot's text is one line (the size the model sees, the full-size file when one was kept, and the coordinate factor for tab.clickAt) instead of four lines with byte sizes and formats, and browser.open reports the tab, the page it landed on when that is not the requested URL (using navigation's normalized URL comparison), and the title on one line (#18776 by @will-bogusz)
  • RPC select requests carry initialIndex, selectionMarker, checkedIndices and markableCount, so hosts can show which ask checkbox options are already checked instead of letting users toggle them blind (#18929 by @andrebrait)
  • Every language server now hands file watching to omp (it was only TypeScript 7); set watchFiles: false on a server to keep its own watcher
  • A failed JavaScript Eval cell prints the error's message and the cell's own frames, with one line counting the hidden frames from omp's runtime, its dependencies and the engine, instead of the full host stack; project-local dependency frames stay visible, cached stacks whose headers no longer match the error stay intact, floating rejections retain their current message and missing-await hint even with a cached stack, and print(e.stack) in a catch still shows every frame (#18775 by @will-bogusz)
  • Snapcompact compaction resolves the frame shape once in its preflight and passes it to compact(), which re-derived it with the auto shape setting; the preflight scans the transcript once instead of twice, runs as the compaction.snapcompact loop phase, and yields to the event loop before rendering (#15689, #18867 by @wolfiesch)
  • Full session file rewrites (pruning, image drops, rescue stamping) run as the session.rewrite loop phase, so their stalls are attributed in ui.loop-blocked logs (#15027, #18867 by @wolfiesch)

Fixed

  • With the Spotlight experiment on, a read with :raw returns the selected bytes instead of an outlined, compacted, collapsed or cut rendering (#18713 by @eggpeat)
  • With the Spotlight experiment on, a ;/, path read is shaped as one call: each part as that read alone would be (an outlined source file beside a :raw part kept verbatim), no part collapsed as already shown against another, and one trace whose notice opens the original (#18713 by @eggpeat)
  • Fixed false unknown-model fallback warnings on cold or stale caches for authenticated openai-codex, google-antigravity, and google-gemini-cli providers, stopped disabled cold-cache providers from deferring fallback warnings, and stopped lazy catalog loads from overwriting completed or failed discovery states (#19017 by @PGupta-Git)
  • ACP clients such as Orca now show an ask HTML form's "Other" text box inside its radio/checkbox question instead of as a second, duplicate question (#19028)
  • Fixed background jobs (&), subshells and command substitutions in the bash tool seeing changes the parent shell made after they started; they now keep the variables, positional parameters ($1, $@), functions, aliases and shell options they started with, as in bash (#19072 by @H4vC; reported in #18990 by @alphastorm)
  • MCP tools returning lists or strings no longer repeat their payload in the model's context when the server also sends SDK-wrapped structured content (#18894, #18920 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • In Chromium browser tabs, page.evaluate, evaluateHandle, $eval, $$eval and waitForFunction inside tab.run now see the page's own globals and custom elements, as tab.evaluate does, instead of null or undefined; waits support concise methods, poll at page-side intervals, reject invalid polling options, preserve abort reasons and stop polling on cancellation, disposing late results (#18751 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed omp bench --account, omp dry-balance --bench, and security scans pinned to one OAuth account sending the raw access token to providers whose API key is a structured credential (GitHub Copilot, Gemini CLI, Antigravity, Snowflake); they now send the same key normal requests do (#19030 by @Xytronix)
  • A hashline PUT whose body repeats the line just outside its range and otherwise leaves the range unchanged now adds that copy, instead of silently doing nothing while reporting an auto-repaired boundary echo (#18234 by @eggpeat)
  • omp --help lists the tool names --tools accepts, grouped into enabled-by-default and optional; browser and computer use are documented as eval features (#19064 by @nibblebot)
  • Added Liquid AI judges (liquid/d1.1, liquid/d1-ultra) for the judge role; images are resized to fit Liquid's per-request patch budget (#18076, #18100 by @SuperUserA)
  • Added Nace Drex judges (nace/drex-max-v1.1, nace/drex-max-finance, nace/drex-v2.0) for the judge role, including Drex Max image judgments (#18950 by @alphastorm)
  • Added the find.hitThreshold setting (above 0, up to 1; unset keeps 0.2): the probability a verified passage needs before find and omp find report its file or list its range, so a judge other than TypeSafe Jev can run at the cutoff that fits its calibration (#18963 by @alphastorm)
  • GitHub Actions watches now show per-step progress, states, and durations, with Ctrl+O expansion and native Tern Surface Protocol views (#18945 by @anatoli-tsinovoy).
  • Computer instructions now describe macOS StaleRef refusals for relabelled controls, including the new ref and the weaker role-and-label recovery candidate from a partial window read (#18892 by @will-bogusz)
  • Computer-use guidance tells the agent to reread the window after UserIntervened on X11 as well as macOS, without undoing or replaying over the user's input (#18784 by @will-bogusz)
  • Computer raise refusals say that on X11 a covered window's screenshot needs a compositing manager, instead of promising one plain X11 cannot supply (#18783 by @will-bogusz)
  • The computer tool's takeover type on Windows no longer loses the end of the text in apps that read keys slowly, such as Chrome, while reporting success, and says in its output when the text was sent but the app was not seen reading it (#19037 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed wait returning "Nothing to wait for" after a directly messaged agent sent an agent://all broadcast before replying, instead of blocking for its direct reply (#18976 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed the TUI marking pending or in-progress todos completed when a subagent with a matching or similar description finishes; matching todos still highlight while the subagent runs and are marked completed only through todo or /todo (#18975 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed worker-thread subagents logging Failed to bind extension … <inline-0> on every spawn and subagents reporting themselves to Warp as separate sessions; an SDK inline extension that worker threads cannot load now logs one warning naming it (#18980 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed the shell's ls builtin so non-numeric -T/--tabsize values (such as ls -laT dir, which previously listed the working directory) and overflowing -w/--width values with trailing non-digits now fail with exit 2 (#18985 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed read JSON queries (archive.tar:member.json?q=.x) on text archive members failing with not found inside archive while preserving literal ? member names and member or query-output line selectors (#18970 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed write LSP diagnostics warning that a #! script is not executable (such as Ruff EXE001) on calls that mark the file executable (chmod +x) (#18978 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed read xd://<tool> and write xd://<tool> for unambiguous wire aliases of enabled tools (such as xd://apply_patch when edit uses apply_patch mode) so they return tool docs, run with the tool's approval tier, and no longer fail with No such tool (#18977 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed yield rejecting a JSON-string data payload with expected object, received string when the decoded value fails the output schema; it now reports the decoded field violations (#18971 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed github run_watch marking failed-job logs unavailable when they contain ANSI or other terminal escape sequences, returning plain-text logs on both current and older GitHub CLI (gh) releases (#18968 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed edit results after lsp.formatOnWrite (or an editor's format-on-save) reformatted the file showing the lines as sent under the formatted file's [path#TAG]; the result now shows the written lines and no longer blames the editor (#18998)
  • Fixed prewalk handing off to the cheaper model on exec-tier xd:// device calls (test runners, remote commands, extension devices) and on proc:// service stdin or mode writes, before any file was changed (#19002)
  • TypeScript 7 (typescript-native) no longer runs its own file watcher: omp watches the files it registers and reports changes, including ones made outside omp, so on Linux it sees edits from other tools instead of keeping stale diagnostics, and on network drives such as ovnfs checkouts its watcher no longer loops, keeping the drive's file server busy and stalling every checkout on it. Other servers opt in with the watchFiles server setting (#18964 by @H4vC)
  • Folders moved into a watched project now report their files to language servers, and folders moved out or renamed report their deletion, so servers no longer keep stale or missing files after mv or a package install
  • Language servers that watch files through omp no longer get changes under node_modules packages or .git object stores unless they asked for those directories, matching VS Code's default watcher excludes
  • On Linux, a project too large for inotify's watch limit is watched one directory at a time instead of losing all file watching, and on network drives a lagging server clock no longer hides changes found after a watcher overflow
  • Fixed the grep tool so directly named files containing NUL bytes are searched as text, directory and glob searches name skipped binary files that match instead of reporting No matches found, and returned match and context lines strip NUL, ANSI escape, and control characters (#18988 by @alphastorm)
  • Added the encourageEdits setting (default on), which makes the system prompt require the edit tool and forbid sed/perl/python file edits; turn it off to drop that guidance.
  • A browser call from Eval that sets no timeout of its own, such as tab.evaluate(…) or tab.run(fn), now runs as long as the Eval cell's timeout instead of failing at 30 seconds, and a call that runs out of time says what happened to the tab and how to give it longer (#18737 by @will-bogusz)
  • Cancelling a browser call now releases an unresponsive worker after its cancellation grace instead of waiting for the full call budget, and recovery after a timeout has its own bounded startup budget (#18737 by @will-bogusz)
  • Cmux browser helpers now use the active browser call's remaining time instead of cutting long evaluations off at the socket's 30-second default or giving late helpers a fresh full timeout; cancelling discards queued helpers and interrupts active socket requests so later calls can proceed (#18737 by @will-bogusz)
  • MCP tools called through write to xd:// devices now honor their device names in model.toolCallLoopGuard.exemptTools, in both the main agent and advisor (#18860)
  • computer.close() no longer disables desktop control for the rest of the session: the next computer call opens a fresh desktop session instead of failing with Computer session is closed
  • An extension provider with an oauth.modifyModels hook no longer loads the whole model catalog at registration while that provider has no login (#18821 by @iliaal)
  • Saving each session entry no longer blocks the UI for several milliseconds on Windows, where on-access scanning delayed the two re-opens of the session file that every write performed (#18819)
  • A retry.fallbackChains model key written with a dotted revision (anthropic/claude-sonnet-5.5) now applies to the model it names (#18951 by @wolfiesch)
  • Sharpshooter consolidation no longer gets stuck retrying every minute once the memory files grow large: the output budget now fits three full files, a cut-off reply is reported as truncated instead of must call replace_memory_files exactly once, and failed runs back off (one interval, doubling up to 6 hours) (#18938)
  • The advisor now backs off between failed turns (doubling per failure, with jitter) and waits for a provider Retry-After hint, instead of retrying every second in step with other sessions (#18957 by @joshuaswarren)
  • In ACP mode, an ask form the client fails to show (e.g. a JSON-RPC -32602 for an unsupported field) now returns the client's error to the agent instead of reporting Ask tool was cancelled by the user and aborting the turn (#18887)
  • omp update runs mise, Homebrew, bun, and npm on the terminal's own output, so their progress bars redraw in place instead of printing every frame as a new line (#18874 by @a-glapinski)
  • Fixed Anthropic prompt caching freezing for the rest of a turn when a context extension rewrites messages the same way on every request (for example a stable tag prefix on each message): every rewritten message was marked per-call, so the rolling breakpoint could not land on anything the tool loop appended and each step re-billed the whole tail as uncached input. A rewritten message is now per-call only once its bytes differ from the previous request; streamed assistant messages are no longer marked per-call just because structuredClone dropped their streaming symbols (#12392, #18869 by @tsankotsanev)

Removed

  • Removed task.file and the task tool's taskFile field; subagent instructions are always inline
  • Todo tasks can no longer be blocked: the blocked status and blocker note are gone from the todo tool, /todo edit ([!] is now an unknown marker), the HUD, ACP plans, goal continuation (which no longer pauses on a blocked-only list) and the RPC TodoStatus/TodoItem wire types and SDKs (Go, Python, Rust). A saved todo list that still holds a blocked task reads back with it pending on resume

@oh-my-pi/pi-mnemopi

Fixed

  • Fixed fact extraction storing decimals, prices, and IP-address fragments as version facts and leaving symbols such as → in metric fact keys (#18886)

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Breaking Changes

  • typeText and axType resolve to { unconfirmed, focusReturn } on every platform instead of the takeover focus report; only Windows takeover typing sets unconfirmed, saying why the target was not seen reading the typed text (#19037 by @will-bogusz)

Added

  • DirectoryWatcher watches a directory on Windows through ReadDirectoryChangesW on its own thread and reports changes in batches with their action (added, removed, modified, renamed) and lost-change signals, without dropping changes during bursts as fs.watch does there (#18964 by @H4vC)
  • DirectoryWatcher changes say whether their entry is a directory, and close() returns without waiting for the watcher thread to cancel its reads (a network round trip on shares)

Fixed

  • Fixed grep() so directly named files containing NUL bytes are searched as text, and directory or glob searches list skipped binary files whose content matches in GrepResult.binaryMatches (#18988 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed computer.apps.open on macOS returning window: null for an HTML file with its own <title>, or a page's window still titled with the previous tab: it now returns the browser window once it shows the page, with the page's title, including without Accessibility permission or when AX window/subrole reads fail; a hidden file-extension display name no longer selects an unchanged unrelated window, and root folders retain immediate display-name matching (#18743 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed the macOS computer tool typing into or pressing a different control than the ref named, and reporting success, once the app reused the control's element (Contacts put the city in the street field): the call now sends nothing and fails with StaleRef, naming the control that now reads as the ref did when there is one (#18892 by @will-bogusz)
  • macOS relabelled-ref recovery also works for refs read from the desktop and searches the owning window once. Ref checks ignore unrelated identity-read failures, skip unused action-name queries, and refuse background input if the user brings its app forward during the check or a later window lookup (#18892 by @will-bogusz)
  • Windows setValue() now detects readable value mismatches and notifies the app for native combo changes without repeating unchanged selections. Hidden or unavailable read-back retains success after a successful write; unsupported combos and unsafe message dispatch are refused, and native messages are timeout-bounded (#18915 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed the Linux computer tool hanging for the rest of the session, and freezing the app, once a press() or menu.select() opened a modal dialog in a GTK 3 app such as Audacity: reads of an app that does not answer now fail with Timeout after 5 s while screenshots and input keep working (#18914 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed responsive Linux AT-SPI windows disappearing when enumeration takes longer than 5 s in total: the timeout now applies to each application reply, not the whole window scan (#18914 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed responsive Linux AT-SPI focus, window-ownership and hit-test scans timing out in aggregate; traversal replies are now bounded individually while single-element reads and mutations retain their operation budgets (#18914 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Wayland desktop accessibility reads selecting a shell surface when the focused application did not answer: shell-only candidates now require confirmed keyboard focus before bypassing the skipped application's timeout (#18914 by @will-bogusz)
  • X11 takeover now observes the first quiet interval, waits for held keys and pointer buttons to be released, drains intervention events before admission, focus changes and further input as well as through focus hand-back, and advertises itself unavailable if observation fails; physical Escape remains observable during keyboard grabs (#18784 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed X11 takeover taking a typing user's keys: a window or element takeover now waits, without holding the input lock, until you have pressed no key or button for 5 s (failing UserIntervened if you are still at it near the run's timeout), and stops with UserIntervened at your first key or click once it has activated its target (#18784 by @will-bogusz)
  • X11 takeover activation failures now ask the window manager to put your window back even when its active-window property has not caught up with core focus, and a press or Escape just after the activation request is sent no longer leaves the window manager switching to the target after the call returns; pointer hand-back no longer warps after your press or while a button is held (#18784 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed X11 window screenshots showing whatever was on screen over the window: under a compositing manager they come from the window's own pixels with its own menus and tooltips, cut to their shapes, and otherwise a covered window is reported covered instead of returning the window on top (#18783 by @will-bogusz)
  • macOS relabelled-ref recovery also works for refs read from the desktop or a display and searches the owning window once. Ref checks ignore unrelated identity-read failures, skip queries for unused action names or child labels, and refuse background input if the user brings its app forward during the check or a later window lookup (#19054 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Linux setValue losing its empty-field warning when a masked field was cleared and the replacement write timed out or was cancelled (#19050 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Linux setValue() with a number reporting success when the control clamped, rounded or ignored it, such as 150 on a 0-100 slider; it now reads the value back and fails naming what the control holds and any bound crossed, without printing an unbounded limit (#19047 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Linux menu.select() refusing every path below a Qt 5 or Qt 6 menu bar ("path traverses a command rather than a submenu") and menu.items() refusing a Qt menu: Qt holds a menu bar item's items in a popup menu, which is now read as that item's submenu, so menu.select(["File", "Save"]) presses the item with no menu opened (#19046 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed a macOS takeover click on a suggestion list, such as Safari's password autofill dropdown, refusing without saying what works: pointer refusals now name the window the list is attached to and say a click without takeover reaches it, without suggesting a click for refused key input (#19027 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed setValue on macOS failing with AxFailed when a password field read back as a mask exactly as long as the text written, although the text had landed (KeePassXC's Qt field); it now succeeds. Typing into such a field by accessibility insertion (el.type, win.type) no longer fails either. A mask of another length still fails (#19026 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed X11 clicks, holds, drags and scrolls on a window, in the background and in takeover, reporting success while that app had a popup menu open, such as one an earlier session opened and left: the menu took the press and closed, and nothing reached the window. Such a call now fails with nothing sent, naming the popup (BackgroundUnavailable in the background, InputFailed in takeover); a press inside the popup or its submenus still goes through (#19044 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Linux X11 background keys and clicks going into the window you were using, while reported delivered, after a screen lock or a switch to another virtual terminal and back (#19023 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Windows takeover typing handing the foreground back before the target had read the typed keys, which sent the end of the text to the user's window while reporting success (a Chrome field kept 218 to 237 of 2,000 characters); typing now waits up to 5 s for the focused control's thread to read them, and unconfirmed says why when it could not see that (#19037 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed setValue errors on macOS and Linux repeating the written text, which could expose a password typed into a field that did not report itself as a password field (#18989 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed clicking an element of a macOS suggestion list, such as a row of Safari's password autofill dropdown, failing with AxFailed: AX element has no identifiable owning window (#19004 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed macOS desktop capabilities claiming globalEscape while Secure Event Input is on (a focused password field, an authentication dialog, a terminal's secure keyboard entry), when macOS hides every key from the Escape and user-input monitors; capabilities now report secureInput and the holding app in secureInputApp (#18982 by @will-bogusz)
  • Windows computer use now reports a locked, disconnected or secure desktop instead of leaving failed captures and undelivered input unexplained (#18947 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed macOS key presses on non-US keyboard layouts pressing the wrong key: on French AZERTY cmd+a quit the app and cmd+z closed the window; shortcuts now use the keys your layout puts those letters on, and a failed layout read stops before sending keys (#18897 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Windows background shortcuts such as ctrl+s and ctrl+a, and Ctrl- or Shift-clicks, reporting success while classic Win32, WinForms and Qt apps got the plain key or click; background input holding Ctrl, Alt, Shift or the Windows key now throws BackgroundUnavailable with nothing sent, and the default backgroundFallback reruns it in takeover (#18895 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed your running Qt 6.5+ apps crashing on Linux X11 when a computer session that used background window input ended or was cancelled, or after an omp process using it was killed (#18825 by @will-bogusz)
  • Limited retained X11 background-input master pairs to two per display, clearing dead holders' focus even while their slaves unplug, reusing free pairs with keyboard locks and latches cleared, and falling back or refusing when both are busy without disabling later attempts; legacy pairs are neither adopted nor counted toward this limit because older omp versions can still remove them (#18825 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed X11 background typing reporting success after losing keys during an X server stall; incomplete typing now fails and disables further background input for the session (#18828 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Windows element typing and key presses that ran in takeover, such as el.type() into a Chrome, Electron or WPF field, leaving the target window in front instead of handing focus back to the user's window; a user window switch before element focus now stops delivery instead of taking focus back (#18919 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed macOS press() on a disabled control reporting success while the control ignored it; it now fails and says the control is disabled (#18898 by @will-bogusz)
  • Windows menu selections that open a modal dialog no longer report a delivery timeout when the new dialog is responsive (#18922 by @will-bogusz)
  • menu.select() and menu.items() on macOS, Linux and Windows now name the items a menu offers, escaping labels and marking disabled items and submenus, when a label in the path is not found; macOS waits for menu revalidation after activation before listing states (#18923 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Linux press() refusing with "AT-SPI action 'press' is unavailable" on Chromium and Electron menu items, list options and tabs, and on page elements whose only action is doDefault: a press now takes their select or doDefault action, as it already took click and activate (#18921 by @will-bogusz)
  • macOS input from a second omp process no longer refuses with UserIntervened after the first process takes over a click in the same app; hardware clicks still pause input in that app (#18896 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed omp worktree add — and the bash tool's git worktree add rewrite — exiting 1 with git: 'hook' is not a git command on Git older than 2.36 (such as Git 2.34 on Ubuntu 22.04) after the worktree had already been created (#18935 by @Bensonluo)

@oh-my-pi/snapcompact

Added

  • resolveShapeAndScan() returns the auto-picked shape together with its scanRenderability() verdict, reusing the scan auto resolution already runs; exported ShapeScan and RenderabilityScan types (#15689, #18867 by @wolfiesch)

Changed

  • compact() splits its synchronous work into two compaction.snapcompact loop phases (building the archive source, then planning the layout) with an event-loop turn between them, shortening the longest main-thread block on long histories (#15689, #18867 by @wolfiesch)

@oh-my-pi/omp-stats

Fixed

  • Spotlight stats count a ;/, path read once with all its parts, and count a later read of any of its files as a re-read or a needed line (#18713 by @eggpeat)

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Added

  • The Spotlight sheet of a ;/, path read shows each part as its own section, numbered from its file's lines with its own ranking (#18713 by @eggpeat)

Fixed

  • Fixed grep tool results in ANSI and native Tern views to show a binary matches warning on both zero-match and matching searches (#18988 by @alphastorm)
  • The /resume picker deletes sessions through the app.session.delete action (default Backspace or Delete) and shows its keys in the hints, so keybindings.yml can replace the shortcut, for example with Ctrl+D, or disable it with []; a bound key that also deletes backward, such as Backspace or Ctrl+W, still edits a typed search query (#17938, #18961 by @wolfiesch)
  • Typing /job lists the jobs command first again instead of jumping whole-hyphen-segment skill matches like skill:…-job-… above it: inner-segment skill hits now tie (rather than outrank) command-prefix matches, so the tie keeps the popup command-first (#18891, #18916 by @yuzu-octopus)

vercel-labs/agent-browser ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.43.0 Stable2026-11-12T23:09:31Z

New Features

  • Added snapshot hints for controls whose accessible name is empty or shared with another ref in the same snapshot. The hint follows the ref and lists the control's icon, image file, data-testid, name or id, and the text of the nearest row or list item (#2578)
  • Added a Modern Web Guidance skill that retrieves Chrome's guidance for a task and checks the built page in agent-browser (#2577)

Bug Fixes

  • Fixed commands on closed tabs waiting for the timeout. A command whose tab closes before Chrome answers now fails with Target closed: CDP session <id> detached (#2576)
  • Fixed recordings with an emulated device scale, which now capture at full density by switching to screenshots when a screencast frame is below the page's scale (#2572)

Improvements

  • Native binaries now ship as per-platform packages, so an install downloads only the binary for the host platform, and the global command links to it when install scripts are blocked (#2150)
  • Snapshots of large pages parse the accessibility tree once, so they finish sooner (#2509)

Documentation

  • Added a Modern Web Guidance guide to the docs (#2546, #2573, #2575)

Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.108graphify 0.9.108 Stable2026-11-12T21:48:11Z
  • Fix: csharp a member call on a receiver typed by a lowercase, underscore-prefixed or keyword-like type (_Pair, pair, a field or primary-constructor parameter so typed) now binds, and a static call like pair.Create() resolves; namespace and unknown receivers still fail closed. Classic extension-method calls on bool/char? receivers also bind (#5174, thanks @JensD-git).
  • Fix: java a call through a class-qualified static field (Service.INSTANCE.get(), and a chain Service.INSTANCE.other().m()) resolves via that field's declared type; a local or parameter that shadows the class name keeps that binding, and an instance field accessed as Type.field is not treated as static (#5149, thanks @MaxWang007).
  • Fix: sql a routine body preserves comma joins (FROM a, b) so every table is read, and keeps a @dblink suffix as a distinct remote node instead of pointing the edge at a local same-named table (#5169, thanks @mohitdixit02).
  • Fix: sql an unparsed derived table's alias (FROM (SELECT ...) q where the subquery fails to parse) is no longer mis-read as a table node; a real table after the subquery is unaffected (#5147, thanks @Yi-111-a).
  • Fix: path graphify path prefers a route through real files over one that detours through a sourceless import hub (an external or stdlib module stub) when both are equally short and confident; when the only route is via a hub it is still returned (#5153, thanks @ayushcodes10).
  • Fix: report GRAPH_REPORT.md keeps the extraction token cost from cost.json after a graphify update, instead of resetting it to zero on the AST-only rebuild path (#5154, thanks @kevin9327).
  • Fix: hooks the search nudge softens when the graph may be stale (pending changes since the last build), mirroring the read nudge, instead of always telling the agent to query the graph; a genuinely unindexed file still gets the plain nudge (#5132, thanks @ayushcodes10).
  • Fix: serve a graph reload no longer stalls every MCP request: the new graph is built off to the side and swapped in atomically, so concurrent requests keep being served the current graph instead of blocking for the whole rebuild, and a single in-flight reload is shared rather than rebuilt per waiter (#5177, thanks @ayushcodes10).
  • Fix: query a --context filter naming a heritage relation (inherits, implements, extends) now reaches heritage edges that carry no context attribute, so the walk follows a class hierarchy instead of stopping at the seed; non-heritage context-less edges are still filtered out, and the header reports when a filter matched no edges (#5178, thanks @sunhouy).
  • Fix: sql Oracle [NON]EDITIONABLE PROCEDURE/FUNCTION/TRIGGER DDL is recovered from the ERROR node instead of yielding only a file node; a table or routine literally named editionable, and non-routine DDL such as EDITIONABLE VIEW, are not mis-recovered (#5167, thanks @Yi-111-a). The AST cache schema is bumped to 45; warm caches re-run the affected C#, Java and SQL extractions.

cli/cli ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.104.0GitHub CLI 2.104.0 Stable2026-11-12T19:03:51Z

Protect private forks when deleting repositories

gh repo delete now warns when deleting a private or internal repository will also delete its forks. The additional confirmation is required even with --yes.

Non-interactive workflows and agents must explicitly accept the cascading deletion:

gh repo delete OWNER/REPO --yes --accept-cascading-deletion-of-private-forks

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v15.18.3 Stable2026-11-12T18:53:05Z

Critical fixes

  • Codex keeps recording after claude-mem updates (#6058, thanks @wiesenthal). Codex approves a hook that ships inside a plugin against a fingerprint of the plugin's whole folder. Every claude-mem update rewrites that folder, so after every update Codex switched all seven claude-mem hooks off ("plugin contents changed; hook approval required") and Codex sessions stopped being saved, with no message. The installer now registers claude-mem's Codex hooks in your own ~/.codex/hooks.json, and each hook runs the on-PATH claude-mem launcher, which finds the newest install by itself. Codex trusts a hook in hooks.json by its own definition and position, so one approval now lasts across updates. Other tools' hooks in that file are kept in place, a second install changes nothing, a hooks.json that can't be parsed is refused and left untouched, and uninstall removes only claude-mem's entries. Turning the claude-mem plugin off in Codex still stops capture. npx claude-mem doctor has a new Codex hooks row that warns when the plugin is on but its hooks are missing from hooks.json.

    Codex users: do this once after updating.

    1. Run npx claude-mem install and select Codex. npx claude-mem update and auto-update don't move the hooks, because older installers saved no agent selection. The installer prints Registered 7 claude-mem hooks in <codex home>/hooks.json.
    2. Open interactive Codex, run /hooks, and approve the seven claude-mem hooks. codex exec can't ask for approval, so do this in interactive Codex first.

    After that, updates don't need a new approval. Codex asks again only when a claude-mem hook's definition changes, when claude-mem adds a hook, or when another tool's hook listed before claude-mem's is removed.

Improvements

  • Optional local API token for the worker API and MCP HTTP transport (#6056, thanks @kartik70, closes #5141). This is for machines with more than one OS user. It is off by default and nothing changes unless you set it. Set CLAUDE_MEM_LOCAL_API_TOKEN in ~/.claude-mem/settings.json, and restart the worker and the MCP HTTP server. After that, the worker API and the MCP HTTP transport refuse any request that doesn't carry the token (X-Claude-Mem-Token or Authorization: Bearer), except the health checks. Open the viewer as /?token=... or /viewer?token=.... The token can only be set in the file: the settings API can't set or clear it, and it is masked when read. omp, hermes and openclaw don't send the token yet. See "Local API Token" in the configuration docs.
  • The quota fallback warning names both providers (#6071, thanks @bulanovk). When the quota fallback moves memory work to another provider, the worker log now names the provider you selected and the one actually doing the work, says when the stand-in spends your Anthropic account, and names CLAUDE_MEM_QUOTA_FALLBACK_PROVIDER=off to turn it off. The default fallback is unchanged.
  • n8n workflow for saving and finding memories (#6066, thanks @Kirat0201, closes #5923). An importable n8n workflow saves a note, checks the worker confirmed the save, then searches and retrieves it within the chosen project. It is inactive until you run it and adds no automatic capture. Setup is in the new n8n docs page.

Fixes

  • smart-read (thanks @Afloat16):
    • Getters and setters with the same name now unfold separately (#6060).
    • Markdown heading levels are kept as written (#6064).
    • Pascal routine bodies are found even when the declaration's case differs (#6063).
    • Python docstrings keep their statement ranges (#6057).
    • Captured source is parsed in native batches (#6059).
  • The observation parser accepts XML whitespace before tag delimiters (#6065, thanks @Afloat16).
  • Globstar exclusions now also apply to paths that contain line terminators (#6061, thanks @Afloat16).
  • Cost report: repository-wide wins are looked up only when asked for explicitly (#6081), and named steering relays are recognized (#6082) (thanks @Kirat0201).
  • The installation guide lists the real Node.js and Bun minimums (#6076, thanks @DeryFerd).

CI and tests

  • The publish and ods-native jobs pin Bun instead of using the newest release (#6075), and CI now runs the hook-io and spawn-env checks (#6074) (thanks @DeryFerd).
  • Windows test fixes: temp-dir cleanup retries, per-platform memory paths, and paused-pipe hook cases skipped where Node can't run them (#6072, #6073, thanks @DeryFerd). The sync-hub test stub is typed so the package typechecks (#6077, thanks @DeryFerd).
  • The #4689 env-fallback test no longer depends on a real Claude login on the machine (#6068, thanks @bulanovk).

View release →

v15.18.2 Stable2026-11-12T17:40:57Z

Critical fixes

  • Gemini CLI on Windows records again (#6079, thanks @tanishqbaweja). On Windows, Gemini CLI runs hook commands through PowerShell. The hook commands claude-mem wrote started with a quoted path, which PowerShell rejects as a parser error. Every claude-mem Gemini hook (SessionStart, BeforeAgent, AfterTool, AfterAgent, SessionEnd) failed on every event since 15.18.0, so Gemini sessions on Windows saved nothing and got no saved context. Hook commands on Windows now start with PowerShell's & call operator, as the Cursor and Windsurf hooks already do. macOS and Linux are unchanged.

    Windows Gemini CLI users: run npx claude-mem gemini install once after updating. npx claude-mem update and auto-update do not rewrite Gemini's hooks, so the broken commands stay until you reinstall them. The reinstall replaces the old commands, and npx claude-mem gemini uninstall removes both forms.

Improvements

  • My Work shows the real state of open work (#6038). Jev now reads each open work-state item once per version on the 10-minute My Work check and labels it: the state its notes describe, plus which of its fields holds the next step and the result. At confidence 0.8 or higher, My Work shows a judged status in place of a quiet agent's stale doing or missing status (finished becomes Done, not checked; waiting on you becomes Needs you; abandoned becomes Closed). A status the agent set on purpose, such as blocked, is kept. Labels are stored in work-view/work-state-labels.json; the work-state log itself is never written. Notes are redacted before Jev reads them, calls are paced one a second, and a service refusal or outage stops the run without being retried until the next check.

CI

  • The integration lane launches Chrome once before its tests, so the first browser test no longer fails on the runner's slow first Chrome start (#6070, thanks @bulanovk, fixes #6069).

rust-lang/rust ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
1.100.0Rust 1.100.0 Stable2026-11-12T18:19:42Z

<a id="1.100.0-Language"></a>

Language

  • Stabilize never type
  • Lint against repeated #[repr(..)] attributes
  • Stabilize extern "custom"
  • Extend dropping_references, dropping_copy_types and undropped_manually_drops lints to drop_in_place
  • unfulfilled_lint_expectations no longer fires on #[expect(dead_code)] inside code that is covered by a dead parent's diagnostic
  • Turn the error for repr(align) fields in a repr(packed) type into a deny-by-default lint aligned_fields_in_packed
  • volatile: allow accesses to non-AM memory to trap

<a id="1.100.0-Compiler"></a>

Compiler

  • Stabilize -Cprofile-sample-use
  • Enable next solver by default in orphanck

<a id="1.100.0-Platform-Support"></a>

Platform Support

  • i686-pc-windows-gnu is now a tier 2 without host tools target
  • Promote wasm32-wasip3 to Tier 2
  • Add new Tier 3 target powerpc64-sony-ps3

Refer to Rust's platform support page for more information on Rust's tiered platform support.

<a id="1.100.0-Libraries"></a>

Libraries

  • Stabilize custom allocators
  • Map ENOTSUP to ErrorKind::Unsupported
  • Fix soundness issues in std::sys::pal::sgx::waitqueue::unsafe_list

<a id="1.100.0-Stabilized-APIs"></a>

Stabilized APIs

  • impl PartialEq<Cow<'_, [U]>> for Vec<T, A>
  • impl PartialEq<Cow<'_, [U]>> for &[T]
  • impl PartialEq<Cow<'_, [U]>> for &mut [T]
  • std::alloc::Allocator
  • std::alloc::Global
  • std::alloc::System
  • impl Allocator for Global
  • impl Allocator for System
  • impl<A: Allocator> Allocator for &A
  • impl<T: Allocator, A: Allocator> Allocator for Box<T, A>
  • impl<A: Allocator> Allocator for &mut A
  • impl<T: Allocator, A: Allocator> Allocator for Arc<T, A>
  • impl<T: Allocator, A: Allocator> Allocator for Rc<T, A>
  • Box::new_in
  • Box::from_raw_in
  • Box::from_non_null_in
  • Box::into_raw_with_allocator
  • Box::into_non_null_with_allocator
  • Box::allocator
  • Vec::new_in
  • Vec::with_capacity_in
  • Vec::from_raw_parts_in
  • Vec::from_parts_in
  • Vec::into_raw_parts_with_allocator
  • Vec::into_parts_with_allocator
  • Vec::allocator

  • impl Default for std::sync::Once
  • bool::toggle
  • Box::take

  • impl AsMut<T> for !

<a id="1.100.0-Cargo"></a>

Cargo

  • Introduced Cargo's own linting system: some Cargo warnings are now emitted as lints that users can control in the [lints.cargo] table. This includes a new built-in unused dependency check via the cargo::unused_dependencies lint. docs #17298
  • Introduced minimum publish age configurations, also known as dependency cooldowns. When the relevant config is set, the resolver ignores versions published more recently than that, unless they are already in Cargo.lock. (RFC 3923) docs #17335
  • cargo-install: now uses the existing lockfile in the .crate tarball by default. --locked in cargo install becomes a no-op. #17388
  • Cargo now uses a new directory layout for intermediate build artifacts. Tools that inspect the build directory may need to use Cargo’s JSON messages to locate build artifacts instead of relying on the build directory layout. The layout is not a stable interface and is subject to change. (build cache doc) #17354

Rustdoc

  • Add new invalid_markdown_table rustdoc lint

<a id="1.100.0-Compatibility-Notes"></a>

Compatibility Notes

  • Closures now more precisely capture variables used in if let. This may cause new errors
  • Check dyn-compatibility of trait objects in more places
  • Infallible is now a type alias to !
  • The never type fallback was changed to ! on all editions
  • box patterns are no longer syntactically allowed
  • Report Query Cycle errors in more cases when computing the layout of enums with explicit discriminants
  • Always escape grapheme extenders in str::escape_debug
  • Make let-else respect macro_rules expr metavariable grouping
  • Iterator::{min,max}(_by_key) changed which Ord methods they use. This cannot change behaviour (other than being potentially faster) for types correctly following the Ord rules, but may impact types with inconsistent trait implementations.
  • Emit an error when trying to enable SSE/AVX on an x86 (32bit or 64bit) softfloat target
  • Require windowed (and exception) for Xtensa ABI
  • windows-gnullvm: always link libunwind statically
  • From version 1.94 up to 1.100 a number of ARMv7-A hardfloat targets required the VFPv3-D32 FPU as a minimum, as they erroneously had the d32 feature in their baseline list of features. Breakage introduced as a result of correcting this can be fixed by passing -Ctarget-feature=+d32 to Rust.
  • Change "Rust" ABI on x86 to use SSE registers if they are always-available for the current target
  • Make the ambiguous_glob_imported_traits lint fire in slightly more cases
  • Make +fix-cortex-a53-835769 a default feature for aarch64-unknown-fuchsia
  • Some impls on Box have been generalised to arbitrary allocators, which may cause new type inference errors
  • The standard library now uses Unicode version 18.0.0
  • WASI SDK version was bumped from 33 to 34
  • On ARM targets, -Ctarget-feature/-Ctarget-cpu values that would alter the ABI are rejected as hard errors now.

<a id="1.100.0-Internal-Changes"></a>

Other

  • Test harness now allows passing --test-threads and --color multiple times, with later arguments overriding earlier ones

View release →

microsoft/skill-recorder ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.2Skill Recorder v0.9.2 Stable2026-11-12T17:57:35Z

Commit-pinned installation

Copy the complete command for your operating system into PowerShell (Windows) or Terminal (macOS/Ubuntu). Use the same command to install or update.

Windows 11 x64 or ARM64

$commit="63bbb50111713b02f3bc0ebbd5f354f4eb038f99"; $env:SKILL_RECORDER_COMMIT=$commit; irm "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/microsoft/skill-recorder/$commit/install.ps1" | iex

Windows now finishes installation without opening the app. Follow the prominent @-bordered completion notice: finish recording, choose Quit in every running Skill Recorder copy, wait for them to close, then open Skill Recorder (Source) from the desktop or Start menu. If shortcut creation fails, use the manual launch command printed by the installer. Reopen the app, not the installer.

macOS or Ubuntu

Quit older running copies before updating, then run:

commit="63bbb50111713b02f3bc0ebbd5f354f4eb038f99"; curl -fsSL "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/microsoft/skill-recorder/$commit/install.sh" | SKILL_RECORDER_COMMIT="$commit" bash

The macOS/Ubuntu installer retains its existing automatic-launch behavior.

For script inspection and hash verification before execution, use the inspect-first instructions. Installer SHA-256 values are listed below.

Fixes

  • Prevent duplicate Skill Recorder instances. Electron's native single-instance lock is acquired before recorder initialization. Another launch reveals the existing recorder or active recording controls instead of creating another main process, tray, and recorder.
  • Restore the existing window. Repeated launches restore a minimized recorder; requests received during startup are handled after initialization. Launching again does not start, stop, or discard a recording, and does not reopen windows during shutdown.
  • Make Windows upgrades explicit. New installs and same-commit reinstalls both finish with manual launch instructions rather than automatically opening another copy. Existing shortcut warnings remain visible, and a safely quoted fallback command is provided when neither shortcut could be created.
  • Update installation documentation and regression coverage. Dependencies and bundled assets are unchanged.

Upgrade and compatibility

  • Quit all older copies before opening 0.9.2. Revisions before 0.9.2 do not participate in the new lock and can still run alongside the new version. The completion notice is an instruction, not automatic detection or shutdown.
  • For revisions that include the fix and share the same application profile, launching a second revision activates the running one. Quit the running copy before opening the newly installed revision.
  • The Windows installer does not scan processes, terminate recordings, or restart the app. SKILL_RECORDER_NO_LAUNCH=1 is no longer needed on Windows; it remains available on macOS/Ubuntu.
  • macOS/Ubuntu source-installer and optional NSIS installer behavior is unchanged. Recordings and application data remain in their existing locations.

Source-only release

Skill Recorder 0.9.2 is published from commit 63bbb50111713b02f3bc0ebbd5f354f4eb038f99.

This release is source-only. No installers or portable binaries are attached. GitHub's generated archives contain the tagged repository source only. Locally assembled source builds are not redistributable release binaries.

Validation

  • 302 local unit/regression/import tests passed.
  • Windows PowerShell 5.1 installer tests passed, including no-auto-launch reuse, the completion banner, shortcut fallbacks, and command escaping.
  • Production build, application/eval type checks, lockfile portability, and the local 199-package license inventory passed.
  • Isolated real Electron launches verified duplicate exit before initialization, minimized-owner restoration, exclusion across versioned entry points, and replacement after graceful quit.
  • Live hotkey activation was not verified because the shortcut was already occupied. No live screen/audio capture or complete legacy installation was performed.
  • Exact release-commit CI passed on Windows x64/ARM64 and macOS/Ubuntu, covering commit-pinned source installation, license/compliance checks, tests, builds, BYOM runtime transport, and applicable packaging/architecture checks. ARM64 packaging passed on rerun after an upstream HTTP 500.

Installer script SHA-256

These hashes were calculated from the exact release commit's Git blobs.

766a2797cff1223266fb56b8c82bb77653dd42dcf3bcc7ffeefa6328bcd8c22d  install.ps1
bd8b5200a2c140c5a5ed9299d565ac72cee5420181774008977eaf75f210db1d  install.sh

Full changelog: https://github.com/microsoft/skill-recorder/compare/v0.9.1...v0.9.2

1.0.3browser-use 1.0.3 Stable2026-11-12T16:52:54Z

pip install 'browser-use==1.0.3'

  • --cli-mcp exits cleanly when the client closed the pipe before the reply (was exit 1 with ExceptionGroup).
  • The CLI runs its own Chromium when no browser has remote debugging on or there is no display, like --cli-mcp. It persists across calls; browser-use --reload closes it.
  • A BU_CDP_URL nothing listens on fails in 3 s with one message naming the value and both ways out (was a 30 s wait).
  • Chrome in another pid namespace (containers, Cursor on Linux) is accepted via its DevToolsActivePort.
  • A cancelled cloud run copies downloads within 10 s instead of holding the browser up to 300 s per pass.
  • ActionResult drops and warns about every ignored 0.x field, not only the first (#6379).

Tencent/BrowserSkill ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
cli-v0.5.0bsk CLI 0.5.0 Stable2026-11-12T16:20:25Z

BrowserSkill CLI 0.5.0 simplifies session management and adds guarded batch actions, reusable recipes, and bounded DOM reads. Use it with extension 0.5.0 for the new observation and interaction features.

Added

  • bsk open, bsk sync, and bsk close manage a browser session for the current caller. Use --scope <name> to isolate parallel tasks. Sync returns short context and observation IDs for subsequent actions.
  • bsk plan runs a sequence of decided actions and stops on the first incomplete step. bsk recipe saves and replays verified workflows, with approval for supported reversible steps and a hold when a previous run's outcome is uncertain. Plan is available in MCP and DSH, and recipe tools are opt-in.
  • bsk read samples bounded DOM fields such as text, attributes, and form state on changing pages. MCP and DSH expose the same read operation. Scripts can declare what they depend on for a narrower pre-action check.
  • The daemon records pending browser actions in a local intent ledger. If an action's effect is uncertain, the ledger holds subsequent writes on that tab until reconciliation. Use bsk sync to inspect current state and bsk ledger status to check the hold.
  • Local pairing verifies the browser and daemon identities and supports credential rotation, recovery, and revocation. Pairing remains optional for local connections. Skill installation also supports Qoder and Qoder CN.

Changed

  • The CLI can apply a text-only observation patch to its exact saved baseline and write the merged observation for offline inspection with bsk view. The daemon and extension also reduce repeated work during synchronization and targeted actions.
  • Native select accepts unique visible option labels. Fill reports when the page reformats the value and redacts the resulting value for sensitive fields.
  • The packaged skill uses the new session commands and documents how to recover when an action's effect is uncertain. DSH documentation covers tool access through run_code in PTC mode.

Fixed

  • Text waits in bsk open and bsk sync evaluate the full observation even when the latest response is a delta or has a token limit.
  • bsk get-html --json --out <file> writes HTML to the file and returns its path and size in JSON.
  • Recording retains its captured steps through session cleanup and better recognizes form controls, shadow DOM selects, and controls whose positions change.
  • Native input calibrates Chromium's clock to reduce false human-intervention events. Navigation waits handle pending navigation and back/forward cache restores.

Upgrade

  1. Finish active browser tasks and run bsk update --yes. If a supervisor manages your daemon, restart it through the supervisor.
  2. Install extension 0.5.0 and update the DSH plugin to 0.5.0 if you use it.
  3. Reload your browser-skill instructions. Restart your MCP client or DSH profile if it still uses the previous version.
  4. Check the connection with bsk --version, bsk status, and bsk doctor.

ext-v0.5.0BrowserSkill Extension 0.5.0 Stable2026-11-12T16:08:16Z

BrowserSkill Extension 0.5.0 improves the browser observations agents use, supports optional local pairing, and fixes shared-control and navigation regressions. Pair it with CLI/daemon 0.5.0.

Added

  • Optional local pairing. Connection settings can verify the daemon and browser identities and recover interrupted pairing. The CLI supports replacing or revoking a paired device.
  • Richer observations. Unlabeled inputs can use nearby captions, native selects show their options, and runs of read-only table rows take fewer lines. Native select can match a unique visible option label.
  • Safer target selection. The extension filters visually hidden instructions from observations while preserving accessible labels. It checks whether a virtualized list has reused the target row before dispatching an action.
  • Collapsible shared-control panel. Minimize the in-page panel to the edge of the tab while a task is active.

Changed

  • When visible text is the only change and the previous observation can be reused, shared-control sync can return a text patch. The extension also reuses recent accessibility data and reduces repeated checks during targeted actions.
  • The extension releases idle debugger connections and reattaches when a browser task needs them. Open dialogs and active downloads keep their connections.
  • Connection settings stay visible in the popup. The pairing control shows the current verification state and offers instructions for pairing or continuing with an unpaired local connection.

Fixed

  • Background fills deliver focus before editing and report page formatting. Sensitive values stay redacted.
  • Navigation waits follow scheduled loads and complete when Chromium restores a page from its back/forward cache.
  • Borrowed blank tabs can navigate or return. Hidden, unresponsive child frames can be reported separately so eligible page actions can proceed.
  • Native input remains correctly attributed after Chromium clock drift, and the control panel preserves click passthrough during status refreshes.
  • Recording captures native select changes in open shadow roots and better recognizes form controls and long button or link names.

Upgrade

  1. Finish active browser tasks and update the CLI/daemon to 0.5.0.
  2. Install extension 0.5.0 from the store or use the ZIP attached to this release. Store availability follows each store's review.
  3. Run bsk doctor to verify the connection. To pair a local browser, run bsk daemon pair and paste the link into the extension's connection settings.

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.31CC Switch v4.0.31 Stable2026-11-12T15:59:54Z

CC Switch v4.0.31

本版本新增受管应用腾讯 WorkBuddy：自定义模型写进 WorkBuddy 自己的模型配置，在 WorkBuddy 里直接就能选；还能在多个 WorkBuddy 账号之间切换，切换前先看到每个号的套餐、剩余积分和到期时间。界面新增德语，README 也有了全部八种界面语言的版本。

另外修复了 Claude Code 经路由连 OpenAI Responses 格式的中转时，会话被同一个中转分到不同 Azure 资源后每一轮都报 400、再也继续不了；经路由连 Gemini Native 格式上游时，并行工具调用报 400、缓存命中率很低；macOS 托盘悬停提示一直挂在屏幕上；Codex 续接的旧对话用量从不导入等问题。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

WorkBuddy

腾讯 WorkBuddy 成为新的受管应用（#8757，需求见 #4802、#5802、#7419、#7076）。和 OpenCode 一样是多个供应商同时生效：

  • 自定义模型：点「添加」后，供应商的模型写进 WorkBuddy 自己的 models.json，在 WorkBuddy 的模型选择里显示为「名称:模型 ID」，名称留空时用「厂商标签」。WorkBuddy 里自己添加的模型原样保留。WorkBuddy 的自定义模型只支持 OpenAI Chat Completions 格式。
    • 预设里的模型在 WorkBuddy 的模型选择里可以选思考强度（例如 Kimi K3.1 Pro 的 low / high / max）。
    • WorkBuddy 暂时不支持同一个模型 ID 出现两次。选预设时会跳过已被别的卡片用掉的模型并说明是哪张卡；手动填了重复的模型 ID 会标出来，不能保存。
  • 官方账号：在 WorkBuddy 里登录过的账号会自动收录到「授权中心」，显示昵称、套餐、剩余积分和积分到期时间（三天内到期标橙）。切换账号有两个入口：添加绑定了这个账号的「WorkBuddy 官方登录」卡片，或者在授权中心这个账号的菜单里点「切换 WorkBuddy 到这个账号」。
    • 切换前要先完全退出 WorkBuddy（包括菜单栏 / 托盘），否则它会把当前登录写回去。
    • 「登录另一个账号」只在本机让 WorkBuddy 停在登录页，不会在服务端登出当前账号，已收录的账号仍然可以切回去。
    • 套餐和积分要在 WorkBuddy 正在运行、并且登录着这个账号时才能查到，查到后会缓存显示。
  • MCP、Skills、提示词：同步到 WorkBuddy 的 mcp.json、Skills 目录和全局提示词 CODEBUDDY.md。WorkBuddy 连接器管理的 MCP 条目不会被改动。
  • 会话管理和用量统计：列出 WorkBuddy 会话，从会话日志导入用量。会话不能在终端里恢复。

德语界面，README 八种语言

  • 德语：首次启动按系统语言自动选择，托盘菜单和快捷面板也已翻译。隐私过滤器的占位符在德语界面下和韩语、俄语一样暂用英文。
  • README 新增繁体中文、韩语、土耳其语和俄语版本，和界面的八种语言一一对应。

其他

  • Skills 页面右上角的「⋯」菜单里直接有「立即重新同步」（#9417）：不用再进「存储与同步」里找。
  • 会话管理「按项目」分组时，Codex 桌面版不属于任何项目的对话归到一组「Codex 聊天」（#9415）：不再每个对话各占一组。
  • 请求日志的状态码筛选新增 403、404、502、503、504、520、529（#9425）。

变更

  • 隐私过滤器改在请求发出前的最后一步过滤（#9460）：以前在请求刚进入路由时过滤，故障转移重试、CC Switch 自己生成的压缩摘要等后来加进请求的内容不会被处理。现在每一次发往上游前都会过滤，覆盖更全；工具的说明文字不处理。检测也更快，结果和以前一致。
  • Codex 用量导入更快（#9400）：读取 Codex 会话日志时少做重复的解析和内存分配。

修复

  • Claude Code / Claude Desktop 经路由连 OpenAI Responses 格式的中转时，偶尔报 400「The requested item was created under a different Azure OpenAI resource」，之后同一会话每一轮都报错（#9424）。中转把同一个会话分到了不同的 Azure OpenAI 资源，会话历史里带着另一个资源生成的思考记录；会话中途换供应商也会报类似的 invalid_encrypted_content。现在上游这样拒绝时，会去掉这些思考记录后自动重试一次，会话可以继续。正常的请求不受影响。
  • Claude Code 经路由连 Gemini Native 格式上游时，并行工具调用后报 400「number of function response parts」（#9453）：分散在几条消息里的工具结果现在会合并到一起再发出。
  • Claude Code 经路由连 Gemini Native 格式上游时，长会话缓存命中率只有两成左右（#9450）：Claude Code 每一轮都会追加一条剩余 Token 提示，以前被挪到整段历史的最前面，导致每一轮的开头都不一样。现在留在原来的位置，前面的历史每一轮保持不变，可以命中缓存。
  • Claude Code 经路由连 Gemini Native 格式上游时，流式请求记录的用量偏少，缓存读取常常记成 0（#9464）。
  • macOS 上托盘图标的「CC Switch」悬停提示一直挂在屏幕上，只能退出应用（#9445、#9329、#9446）：macOS 上不再显示这个提示，出问题时图标上的圆点和快捷面板照常提醒。
  • macOS 上其他应用全屏时，点菜单栏图标弹不出快捷面板（#9331）。
  • Claude Code 路由模式下，供应商里设置的 CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL_FORCE 写不进 settings.json（#9451、#9455）：现在和供应商的其他专属设置一样随供应商写入，你自己在全局设置的值也不会被切换清掉。
  • Codex 续接已结束的旧对话后，新产生的用量一直没有导入（#9420）。
  • Codex 直连第三方供应商时，关掉「非接管切换时保留官方登录」不会立即生效，要切回官方卡再切回来（#9367）：现在改完设置就会重写当前供应商的配置。
  • Codex 第三方供应商里与官方同名的 GPT 模型，模型选择里显示的不是你设置的显示名（#9380）。
  • macOS 上卸载 Claude Desktop 后，「应用」页仍把它列为已安装（#9383）：现在只认 Claude.app，残留的数据目录不再算。
  • Linux KDE Wayland 上复制按钮提示成功，但剪贴板是空的或还是旧内容（#9384）。
  • Copilot CLI 官方卡的账号下拉里，不绑定账号的选项写着「使用默认账号」，实际用的是 Copilot CLI 自己的 GitHub 登录（#9408）：现在写成「跟随 Copilot CLI 自己的登录」。
  • DeepSeek Harness 手动添加的模型没法设置兼容选项（#9330）：现在也显示兼容性编辑器，可以为百炼兼容模式这类不接受 developer 角色的接口关掉这个角色。
  • 启用 Skill 时提示符号链接创建失败，看不出具体原因（#9439）：报错里现在带上系统给出的错误码（例如 Windows 的 os error 5），方便判断是权限问题还是别的原因。
  • 侧栏底部的「应用」和「设置」，德语、土耳其语、俄语界面下文字被截断。

升级说明

数据库版本从 25 升到 26，为 MCP 和 Skills 增加 WorkBuddy 的开关。升级前会自动备份数据库；升级后再退回旧版本，旧版本会提示数据库版本过新，打不开。

致谢

感谢 @Owlbay（WorkBuddy）、@allenxu09（隐私过滤器改在出口过滤、Codex 用量导入提速）、@jinyule（macOS 托盘悬停提示）、@zhjyz2（CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL_FORCE）的贡献。

感谢 @CookieCN、@yanglei9491-hue、@pankejia2000、@wengdongyang、@cooper1x、@lemonight、@qmy-it、@SunflowerNocturne、@WuFan-sl、@Ax-For、@2489055825、@njwangcn、@hjingsuper、@IT-coder-Yy、@hqing8156-prog、@CrazyFigure、@declandragon、@zhengzp2019、@TOBYmiz、@JiangHe12 提出需求、报告问题和提供复现信息。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。
